Without fine-tuning, it is the most accurate on DL3DV and has the best PSNR and SSIM on ScanNet++. The code will be released at https://github.com/Jingong-Chen/GradSplat/.

</details>


### [68] [What do MLLMs know about 'Waldo'? Evaluating Visual Reasoning under Multi-Instance Multi-Object Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38957)
*Saumya Mundra,Ajoy Mondal,Vineeth N Balasubramanian,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MIMO-Bench，一个面向"多物体类别、每类多实例"（MIMO）场景的多模态大模型视觉推理基准，包含 3,802 个图文问答对，涵盖属性绑定与计数任务，揭示现有 SOTA 模型在相似实例干扰下的显著性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基准中，每类物体实例数很少，目标指代过于简单且视觉歧义有限，无法真实检验 MLLM 在需要多重视觉子任务组合才能唯一定位目标时的推理能力。

Method: 构建 MIMO-Bench：图像-问题对均涉及含多实例的物体类别，利用相似特征干扰项迫使指代表达需由多个视觉子任务组合以无歧义定位目标；共 3,802 个图文对，覆盖属性绑定与数值计数两类任务，并评测开源（InternVL、QwenVL）与闭源（Gemini、GPT-5）模型。

Result: SOTA 开源与闭源模型在现有基准上表现优异，但在 MIMO-Bench 上性能显著下降，暴露出其在多实例干扰场景下的视觉推理局限。

Conclusion: MIMO 设置构成对现有多模态模型的有效挑战，MIMO-Bench 可弥补现有基准在实例密度与视觉歧义上的不足，并将公开可用。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to significant progress in visual reasoning capabilities. However, existing benchmarks curate image-question pairs pertaining to object categories with few instances, enabling trivial object referencing and limited visual ambiguity. In this work, we introduce MIMO-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs in a Multi-Instance per Multi-Object categories (MIMO) setting. Each image-question pair in our benchmark pertains to instances belonging to object categories with multiple instances. The presence of such similar feature distractors causes hindrance in basic visual reasoning tasks, necessitating the referring expression to be composed of multiple visual subtasks to uniquely and unambiguously identify the target object. MIMO-Bench comprises 3,802 image-question pairs across attribute binding and numerical counting tasks. Despite impressive performances on existing benchmarks, both SOTA open-source (InternVL, QwenVL) and proprietary (Gemini, GPT-5) models exhibit substantially lower performance on MIMO-Bench, highlighting their limitations in the MIMO setting. The benchmark will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [69] [AlbumVSR: Identity-Preserving Face Video Super-Resolution from a Personal Photo Album](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39001)
*Nianyao Du,Xijun Wang,Stanley H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumVSR 用同一人物相册作为外部身份先验，通过冻结的单步流式扩散 Transformer 实现严重退化下的人脸视频超分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严重模糊、下采样、噪声与压缩会抹除忠实恢复人脸所需身份线索，需引入外部身份源。

Method: 在个人相册上条件化一个冻结的单步流式扩散 Transformer；用 4 个 MagFace/CLIP 集合 token 编码相册，并用按当前窗口估计人脸姿态渲染的融合 UV 图集生成 64 个纹理 token，追加到缓存的交叉注意力上下文。

Result: 身份不相交的 VoxCeleb2 重退化基准上，ArcFace 相似度由 0.586 升至 0.670，人脸 PSNR 由 24.98 升至 26.71 dB；91 个测试身份上的平均身份相似度超过全部 14 个基线。

Conclusion: 以相册为先验的条件化 token 设计可在冻结骨干上有效提升重度退化人脸视频的身份保真度。

Abstract: Severe blur, downsampling, noise, and compression can erase the identity cues needed to faithfully restore a person's face. We therefore use photographs of the same person as an external source of identity. AlbumVSR conditions a frozen, one-step streaming diffusion transformer on a personal album. We use four MagFace/CLIP set tokens to encode the album, and 64 texture tokens which come from a fused UV atlas rendered at the current window's estimated face pose. These tokens are appended to the transformer's cached cross-attention context. On an identity-disjoint VoxCeleb2 benchmark with heavy synthetic degradation, AlbumVSR raises ArcFace similarity to the ground truth from 0.586 to 0.670 and face PSNR from 24.98 to 26.71 dB. On the 91 test identities, its mean identity similarity is higher than that of all 14 baselines.

</details>


### [70] [The Search for Statistical Indistinguishability Between Real and Fake Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39014)
*Mohaimin Rafi,Otavio Moraes Rosa,Anshuman Chhabra,Utkarsh Ojha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统检验生成图像是否已在统计上不可区分于真实图像：在多种渐进抽象（模糊、缩放、裁剪、边缘/语义图）下训练判别网络测试其准确率。结果显示几乎没有任何抽象能让伪造数据变得不可区分，除非图像内容被抽象到人眼几乎无法识别的程度，说明伪造痕迹从低层一直延伸到高层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型产出的图像在视觉上已与真实图像高度相似，因此需要回答一个更严格的问题：生成内容是否已在数据属性层面达到"完美"，即真正与真实数据统计不可区分。仅当判别器表现退化为随机水平时，才能作为统计不可区分的证据。

Method: 训练深度神经网络区分真实与伪造数据并观测其测试准确率；考虑到生成模型常引入低层伪影（如棋盘格伪影），对原始真假图像施加一系列渐进抽象操作（模糊、降采样、裁剪、将 RGB 转为边缘图/语义图等），期望在某个抽象空间中伪影被抹除，从而检验可分性是否消失。实验覆盖四个复杂度不同的数据集（MNIST、CelebA、FFHQ、GenImage）以及多种生成模型类型（GAN、VAE、扩散模型）。

Result: 几乎所有抽象方式都无法使伪造数据与真实数据不可区分；分类器仅在数据被抽象到内容对人眼基本丢失的程度时（例如将 256×256 的人脸 RGB 图像降采样到 4×4）才失效。该趋势在四个数据集与各类生成模型上一致成立。

Conclusion: 伪造伪影的本质跨越了从低层到高层的全部图像特征层级，而非仅限低层纹理瑕疵；这意味着生成图像与真实图像的差异远比通常认为的更大，生成内容在数据属性层面尚未达到"完美"。

Abstract: Images produced by generative models appear strikingly similar to real ones. In this work, we study whether perfection has been reached by generated content in terms of matching the properties of real data. We train deep neural networks to separate real data from fake and observe their test accuracies. Only a chance performance has any scope of proving statistical indistinguishability. However, since generative models are known to sometimes introduce small errors, e.g., low level checkerboard artifacts, we create progressive abstractions of the original real/fake images to potentially bring them to a space where those artifacts get wiped away. These include operations such as blurring, downsizing, cropping, converting RGB images into edge/semantic maps, etc. To our surprise, almost none of the abstractions are able to make fake data indistinguishable from real. The only cases when neural network based classifiers fail to separate the two categories is when the data is abstracted to such an extent that the original content seems mostly lost to the human eye, e.g., downsizing a $256 \times 256$ RGB image of human face to $4 \times 4$ resolution. We observe this trend across four different datasets of varying complexity (MNIST, CelebA, FFHQ and GenImage) and across various types of generative models (GANs, VAEs, diffusion models). This shows that the nature of fake artifacts extends all the way from low level to very high level in images. The simple implication is that fake images are much more different from real than we are used to think.

</details>


### [71] [LightGenBench: A Benchmark for 3D Emission Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39038)
*Dongchen Yang,Xingguang Yan,Manolis Savva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"发光生成"(emission generation)新任务：由3D资产的几何、非发光材质与一张渲染视图，预测其表面空间变化的RGB自发光。为此构建LightGenBench基准，含37k艺术家制作发光资产（UV/体素/多视图三种形式）、四类方法基线及评估协议；最优稀疏体素基线TRELLIS.2-Emission仅达IoU 0.271，表明任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成方法能产出细节几何与丰富纹理的资产，但这些资产均为"非发光"的——只反射光而不发光。作者希望将发光属性纳入3D生成范畴，使生成资产具备自发光能力，从而需要预测表面空间变化的发光分布，这一方向此前缺乏任务定义与评测平台。

Method: (1) 构建数据集：37k张艺术家制作的发光资产，提供UV、体素、多视图三种表示形式；(2) 建立四类方法族基线：UV域生成、稀疏体素生成、多视图生成、基于分割的生成；(3) 设计评估协议：度量"在哪里发光"与"如何发光"，并结合不同发光强度渲染图的用户研究比较。

Result: 四类基线中稀疏体素方案TRELLIS.2-Emission表现最佳，因其直接在3D原生空间生成发光，规避了UV空间的碎裂(fracturing)伪影与多视图生成无法看到的被遮挡发光体问题。但其IoU仅0.271，任务远未被解决。

Conclusion: 原生3D生成发光优于UV与多视图表示，但整体性能仍很低；作者希望LightGenBench将发光生成确立为3D生成领域可基准化(benchmarkable)的问题，推动后续研究。

Abstract: Recent generation methods produce 3D assets with detailed geometry and rich textures. However, these assets are non-emissive: they reflect light but emit none. We study a new task, emission generation: predicting spatially varying RGB emission over a 3D asset's surface from its geometry, non-emissive materials, and a rendered view.
  To support this task, we introduce LightGenBench. Our benchmark makes three contributions. First, we curate a dataset of 37k artist-authored emissive assets in UV, voxel, and multi-view form. Second, we establish baselines across four method families: UV-domain, sparse-voxel, multi-view, and segmentation-based generation. Third, we design an evaluation protocol that measures where an asset emits and how, complemented by user-study comparisons on renderings with varying emission strength.
  Across the four families, the sparse-voxel baseline TRELLIS.2-Emission performs best because generating emission natively in 3D avoids the failure modes of the other representations, such as the fracturing artifacts of UV space and the occluded emitters that multi-view generation cannot see. However, TRELLIS.2-Emission only reaches an IoU of 0.271, leaving the task far from solved. We hope LightGenBench establishes emission generation as a benchmarkable problem for 3D generation.

</details>


### [72] [Kinematic Causal Attribution: Black-Box Explanations of Motion versus Geometry for 4D Point-Cloud Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39147)
*Zerui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KCA是一个黑盒、干预式的4D点云视频归因方法，通过"冻结运动/剥离形状"算子构建运动×几何立方体并做Shapley分解，在三个骨干上区分运动与形状线索，且不损失忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D感知模型（点云视频分类）将被部署到机器人、辅助监护与AR中，但实践者在部署前无法检验模型依赖的是哪种线索（身体部位的运动或形状）。现有归因方法存在局限：3D归因仅限单帧，视频归因局限于RGB像素，梯度图需白盒访问，注意力机制在状态空间骨干中缺失，点级扰动依赖跨帧点对应而真实深度/LiDAR数据不具备。

Method: 提出Kinematic Causal Attribution（KCA）：黑盒、干预式，仅查询模型输出。冻结算子移除区域运动但保留其形状，无形状代理移除形状但保留运动；二者为每个被跟踪区域张成运动×几何立方体，采用顺序对称的Shapley分解（完整）并显式测量交互项而非忽略。算子重写逐帧点集，无需点对应；上下文平均估计器处理跨多区域的结构。

Result: 在MSR-Action3D上训练的官方PSTNet、P4Transformer、Mamba4D上验证。审计实验：用真实片段构建两个二分类任务，两者仅在"同一手臂"的运动或形状上不同，手臂重要性的运动占比随定义线索变化（P4Transformer 0.83 vs 0.60，PSTNet 0.86 vs 0.30，Mamba4D 0.92 vs 0.61），此区分单张归因图无法做到。忠实性无损：必要性图在P4Transformer与PSTNet上取得最低删除AUC，在Mamba4D上与最佳基线持平，且权重随机化后与训练模型的相关性消失。此外完成骨架真值、重采样稳定性与开销验证。

Conclusion: KCA提供了无需对应、黑盒可用的4D点云归因，可完整分解运动与几何贡献并显式量化二者交互，成功在多种骨干上区分运动与形状线索，同时保持忠实性，适用于部署前的模型审计。

Abstract: 4D perception models, which classify 3D point-cloud videos, are being built into robots, assistive monitoring and AR, yet before deploying one a practitioner cannot check which cue it relies on: how a body part moves, or what shape it has. 3D attribution is single-frame, video attribution is confined to RGB pixels, gradient maps need white-box access, attention is absent from state-space backbones, and point-level perturbations assume a point correspondence across frames that real depth and LiDAR data lack. We present Kinematic Causal Attribution (KCA), a black-box, interventional attribution method that queries only the model's outputs. Its freeze operator removes a region's motion while keeping its shape, and a shape-free proxy removes the shape while keeping the motion. Together they span a motion$\times$geometry cube for every tracked region, whose order-symmetric Shapley decomposition is complete and whose interaction is measured rather than assumed away. The operators rewrite per-frame point sets, so they need no correspondences, and a context-averaged estimator handles structures that span several regions. We validate KCA on the official PSTNet, P4Transformer and Mamba4D trained on MSR-Action3D. The decisive test is an audit: two binary tasks are built from real clips whose classes differ only in the motion or only in the shape of the same arm, and the motion share of the arm's importance follows the defining cue on all three backbones (0.83 vs. 0.60 on P4Transformer, 0.86 vs. 0.30 on PSTNet, 0.92 vs. 0.61 on Mamba4D), a distinction no single attribution map can make. The split costs no faithfulness: the necessity maps have the lowest deletion AUC on P4Transformer and PSTNet, tie with the best baseline on Mamba4D, and lose their correlation with the trained model's once the weights are randomised. Skeleton ground truth, re-sampling stability and cost complete the validation.

</details>


### [73] [STRATA: Reinforcement-driven Active Sampling with Tri-paradigm Adaptation for Efficient 3D Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39151)
*Junchao Zhu,Ruining Deng,Chongyu Qu,Zhengyi Lu,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Yu Wang,Shilin Zhao,Haichun Yang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRATA：一种强化学习驱动的主动采样框架，通过三范式自适应预测器，从稀疏体素级ST采样重建密集3D转录组图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D体转录组ST采集成本高昂；AI方法精度有限；现有2D逐层ST仍依赖密集网格采样，导致测量冗余与生物信息区域欠采样。

Method: 构建融合H&E形态与单细胞先验的3D图；用强化学习智能体估计形态稀疏性与转录组信息量，实现预算感知的信息性点选择；再用自适应三范式预测器，通过轻量融合结合检索、回归与跨层插补，并动态加权。

Result: 多数据集实验表明采样效率与精度提升，尤其在低预算设定下表现突出。

Conclusion: STRATA以主动采样+三范式预测弥合稀疏体素获取与密集3D图谱重建之间的差距，在低成本下实现高效准确的3D转录组重建。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables joint characterization of tissue morphology and gene expression. However, 3D volumetric ST acquisition remains expensive. AI-driven methods offer a low-cost alternative but remain limited in accuracy, while recent 2D section-wise ST acquisition still rely on dense grid sampling, resulting in redundant measurements and undersampling of biologically informative regions. To bridge the gap, we propose STRATA, a reinforcement-driven active sampling framework with tri-paradigm adaptation for reconstructing dense 3D transcriptomic maps from sparse voxel-wise ST acquisition. STRATA constructs a 3D graph integrating H&E morphology with single-cell priors and employs a reinforcement learning agent to estimate morphological sparsity and transcriptomic informativeness, enabling budget-aware selection of informative spots. Given these sparse measurements, an adaptive tri-paradigm predictor combines retrieval, regression, and cross-layer imputation through lightweight fusion, dynamically weighting their contributions. Experiments across datasets demonstrate improved sampling efficiency and accuracy, particularly under low-budget settings. Code is available at https://github.com/Junchao-Zhu/STRATA.

</details>


### [74] [VEvo-Harness: Causally-Guarded Self-Evolving Harness for Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39158)
*Yuan Wang,Yuhang Yu,Mengting Chen,Tianrun Xu,Zihao Pan,Xuetao Feng,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VEvo-Harness，首个面向智能体视觉生成的因果守卫式持续自演化框架，通过视觉回溯、视觉持续治理、生命周期化状态管理与异构沙箱策略，把浅层“生成-验证-重写”循环升级为可治理的持续学习系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成与编辑的瓶颈已从像素级保真转向满足密集约束的用户规格，而现有智能体流程仍是浅层 generate-verify-rewrite 循环：把 VLM 判断当作粗糙视觉反馈，要么丢弃修复经验，要么不加门控地更新从而污染技能库；由于缺乏记忆与治理阶段，系统退化为无治理的反复试错迭代器，而非持续自演化系统。

Method: 四项耦合创新：(1) 视觉回溯（visual traceback），用分层验证树将稀疏判断稠密化为根因归因；(2) 视觉持续治理，通过反事实双臂图像生成重放，仅接纳因果有效的技能补丁；(3) 生命周期化的 harness 状态，在 working/experience/pattern 层级上分层蒸馏以根因锚定的 episode，形成可路由、经因果验证的持久技能库；(4) 异构沙箱策略，编排多样修复原语动作空间，依据根因进行成本感知的动态路由以选出最优干预策略。

Result: VEvo-Harness 超越当前最先进的智能体方法，并可媲美专有生成器（如 GPT-Image、Nano-Pro），同时保持持续的 harness 演化与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将验证反馈的因果化、技能更新的门控化与经验记忆的生命周期化管理结合，可使智能体视觉生成从无治理试错转向可治理的持续自演化，兼具性能与泛化优势。

Abstract: The central bottleneck in visual generation and editing has migrated from achieving pixel-level fidelity to satisfying densely constrained user specifications through increasingly agentic workflows. Prevailing agentic generation pipelines remain shallow generate-verify-rewrite loops treating VLM judgments as coarse visual feedback, either discarding repair experience or admitting ungated updates that poison skill library. With memorize and govern periods left open, they degrade into ungoverned iterators of repeated trial-and-error rather than continual self-evolving harness. Towards this issue, we present \textbf{VEvo-Harness}, the first causally-guarded continual self-evolving harness system for agentic visual generation. VEvo-Harness introduces four coupled innovations: (1) \textit{a visual traceback} that densifies sparse judgments into root-cause attributions with a hierarchical verification tree; (2) \textit{a visual continual governance} that admits only causally valid skill patches through counterfactual dual-arm image generation replay; (3) \textit{a lifecycle-managed harness state} that hierarchically distills root-cause-anchored episodes across working, experience, and pattern tiers into a persistent skill library of routable and causally verified capabilities; (4) \textit{a heterogeneous sandbox policy} that orchestrates a diverse action space of repair primitives, dynamically routing cost-aware interventions based on root causes to select the most effective strategy. VEvo-Harness surpasses state-of-the-art agentic methods and rivals proprietary generators (\textit{e.g.}, GPT-Image, Nano-Pro) while maintaining the continual harness evolution and generalization.

</details>


### [75] [CLaW: Codec-Guided Adaptive Latent Watermarking for Traceable Diffusion Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39160)
*Huangchao Song,Lingyun Yu,Cong Li,Peiqi Jiang,Mingqi Fang,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLaW框架，用预训练水印编解码器将消息映射为潜空间残差，在去噪后期窗口自适应注入，实现免反演检测的高鲁棒性扩散图像水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型水印难以规模化部署：需昂贵重训练或检测开销大、有效性—保真度折中差、对图像变换鲁棒性有限，且缺乏用户级可溯源能力。

Method: 冻结扩散主干、免反演检测；1) 预训练水印编解码器将每位水印消息映射为潜空间残差，实现低成本采样期注入；2) 在后期去噪窗口注入，此时语义已较好保留且信号受后续去噪扰动小；3) 解码器引导的自适应注入机制，以解码置信度为反馈动态校准水印强度，增强弱信号。

Result: 大量实验表明CLaW保持高视觉保真度，并在图像变换下取得当前最优鲁棒性，可支持可靠的用户级溯源。

Conclusion: CLaW以冻结主干+编码—解码引导的潜空间后期注入路径，兼顾保真度与鲁棒性，为扩散生成图像的可溯源水印提供了可扩展的高效方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of text-to-image diffusion models, increasingly realistic AI-generated content has raised serious concerns about misuse and copyright infringement. Digital watermarking offers a promising solution by enabling user-level traceability, yet existing methods remain difficult to deploy at scale, hindered by costly model retraining or detection, poor effectiveness-fidelity trade-offs, and limited robustness to image transformations. To address these challenges, we propose CLaW (Codec-guided Latent Watermarking), a robust and efficient watermarking framework for traceable diffusion image generation with frozen diffusion backbones and inversion-free detection. Specifically, CLaW first uses a pretrained watermark codec to map each watermark message to a latent residual, enabling low-cost sampling-time injection. Then, the residual is injected within a late denoising window, where image semantics are better preserved and the watermark signal is less perturbed by subsequent denoising updates, yielding a better balance between watermark effectiveness and image fidelity. Furthermore, we introduce a decoder-guided adaptive injection mechanism that uses decoding confidence as feedback to dynamically calibrate watermark strength, reinforcing weak watermark signals and improving robustness under image transformations. Extensive experiments show that CLaW preserves high visual fidelity and achieves state-of-the-art robustness under image transformations, enabling reliable user-level traceability.

</details>


### [76] [Joint Rigid Alignment and Morphable Reconstruction from Partial 3D Face Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39161)
*Eli A. Underwood,Nicolas Rondan,Angel V. Peterchev,J. Matias Di Martino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单一网络，在GeoTransformer基础上一次前向同时预测刚性位姿与形状完整点云：由交叉注意力回归器预测3DMM逐块系数生成形变模板，再以其为目标做Sinkhorn精匹配与刚性位姿估计，仅用几何对齐监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分真实3D人脸扫描对齐到规范表示需同时解决刚性位姿估计与未观测区域形状补全，而现有流程将两者解耦（通用点云配准前端+独立非刚性拟合/形变），步骤分离、效率低且易误差累积。

Method: 基于GeoTransformer超点匹配架构构建单一网络；交叉注意力回归器以Transformer点特征和预测的粗对应为条件，将部分扫描映射为离线从8000个身份语料学得的3DMM逐patch系数；同一前向中用形变模板作为细粒度Sinkhorn匹配与刚性位姿估计的目标；监督仅来自与真值形变几何的几何对齐，无需人工标注系数。

Result: 在留出身份上，相比六种经典与学习的非刚性配准基线，逐顶点误差领先最强基线3-4倍，对将头部塌缩到可见区域的基线领先一个数量级以上；在NoW与NPHM两个独立真实扫描数据集的无真值扫描上同时取得最高精度与最快速度。

Conclusion: 将位姿估计与形状补全统一为端到端单次前向预测是可行且有效的，几何对齐监督足以替代人工系数标注，方法在精度与效率上均优于现有非刚性配准流水线。

Abstract: Aligning a partial, real-world 3D face scan to a canonical representation requires solving two coupled problems at once: estimating the rigid pose that brings the scan into a common frame, and completing the facial shape in regions the scan never observed. Existing pipelines typically decouple these steps, running a generic point-cloud registration front end followed by a separate non-rigid fitting or deformation stage. Building on the GeoTransformer superpoint-matching architecture, we propose a single network that predicts both a rigid pose and a shape-complete point cloud in one forward pass: a cross-attention regressor, conditioned on transformer point features and predicted coarse correspondences, maps a partial scan to per-patch coefficients of a 3D morphable face model learned offline from a corpus of 8,000 identities. The resulting morphed template is then used, in the same forward pass, as the target for fine-grained Sinkhorn matching and rigid pose estimation, supervised purely by geometric alignment to ground-truth morphed geometry, so no hand-labeled coefficients are required. Against six classical and learned non-rigid registration baselines on held-out identities, our method leads the strongest ones by 3-4 times on per-vertex error and baselines that collapse the head onto the visible region by more than an order of magnitude, and is simultaneously the most accurate and fastest method on ground-truth-free scans from two independent real-scan datasets, NoW and NPHM. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [77] [Edited Endpoints as Partial Goals for Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39179)
*Sihyun Kim,Sanghyun Jo,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 编辑后端点图像会因保留源图布局上下文而使全帧端点条件化引发时间伪影；提出仅约束编辑相关区域的Partial条件化方案，在三个骨干网络上降低时间伪影且不损害转场完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑后端点图像虽能显式化图像到视频转场目标并提升转场完成度，但同时保留了源图布局上下文；全帧端点条件化将这些上下文变成对视频最终状态的硬性要求，可能诱发时间伪影，作者希望探究伪影的成因与缓解方式。

Method: 用Hard-Cut(HC)衡量不连续性、Background Motion Reversal(BMR)衡量后期背景反转、Dynamic Degree(DD)衡量运动抑制；在跨越免训练与学习式端点条件化的五个视频模型上系统观察伪影。提出Partial选择性条件化：仅约束由图像编辑注意力估计出的编辑相关区域，其余终端内容交给视频模型自由生成。并在三个骨干网络上于TC-Bench-I2V评测，辅以均匀弱化条件的对照与"供体上下文"干预实验（保留全帧条件，仅将编辑相关区域外的上下文替换为Partial视频的末帧）。

Result: 在三个骨干网络上，Partial相对全帧条件化显著降低时间伪影，TCR无显著差异；在Wan2.2-Turbo上均匀弱化条件化无法复现上述降低。供体上下文干预在HunyuanVideo-1.5上降低HC与BMR，在Wan2.2-Turbo上降低BMR并恢复DD，且TCR无显著差异。

Conclusion: 伪影不能仅由约束范围解释；编辑后端点应作为"部分未来目标"使用，但编辑相关性并不保证未来有效性。代码与数据将于 https://github.com/wannagetacat/Edited-Endpoints-I2V 发布。

Abstract: Edited endpoint images make the target of an image-to-video transition explicit and can improve transition completion, but also preserve context in the source image's layout. Full-frame endpoint conditioning turns this preserved context into requirements on the video's final state, potentially inducing temporal artifacts. We measure discontinuity with Hard-Cut (HC), late background reversal with Background Motion Reversal (BMR), and motion suppression with Dynamic Degree (DD). These artifacts appear across five video models spanning training-free and learned endpoint conditioning, in model-specific forms. We study Partial, a selective conditioning scheme that constrains only an edit-relevant region estimated from image-editor attention and leaves the remaining terminal content to the video model. Across three backbones on TC-Bench-I2V, Partial reduces temporal artifacts relative to full-frame conditioning, with no significant difference in Transition Completion Ratio (TCR); on Wan2.2-Turbo, uniformly weakening conditioning does not reproduce these reductions. A donor-context intervention keeps full-frame conditioning and replaces only the context outside the edit-relevant region with the final frame of a Partial video. It lowers HC and BMR on HunyuanVideo-1.5 and lowers BMR while restoring DD on Wan2.2-Turbo, with no significant difference in TCR, so constraint extent alone does not account for these artifacts. Together, these findings suggest that edited endpoints should serve as partial future goals, although edit relevance does not guarantee future validity. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/wannagetacat/Edited-Endpoints-I2V.

</details>


### [78] [Multi-level Constrained Learning for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39183)
*Wangtao Bao,Liping Xie,Kanjian Zhang,Huimin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MCL-UIE配对监督水下图像增强框架，通过约束颜色映射、细节修正强度与观测一致性提升增强质量，在16项全参考对比中13项第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像因波长相关衰减、散射与噪声导致颜色偏差与细节退化。配对参考监督仅约束输出相似性，本身无法明确颜色映射方式、局部细节修正强度以及与退化观测的一致性。

Method: 包含三个组件，共享一个恢复网络：(1) 单调色度变换MCT，在log-LMS空间用正对角项与有界跨通道系数约束全局颜色映射；(2) 不确定性门控小波重建UGWR，用基于归一化小波重建误差校准的学习置信门调制高频残差；(3) 参考锚定退化重放RADR，用共享潜在退化代理对增强图与配对参考同时渲染，促使二者可复现输入；RADR仅训练时使用，无推理开销。

Result: 在16项全参考数据集-指标对比中13项排名第一；无参考基准上取得有竞争力的视觉效果。消融显示置信校准在与重放监督结合时最有效。

Conclusion: 通过结构化颜色校正、局部可控细节恢复与参考锚定重放的统一框架，可有效满足配对监督下颜色映射、细节修正强度与退化一致性的要求，且不增加推理成本。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement aims to correct color and recover detail degraded by wavelength-dependent attenuation, scattering, and noise. Paired-reference supervision constrains output similarity, but does not by itself specify the color mapping, the strength of local detail corrections, or consistency with the degraded observation. We propose MCL-UIE, a paired-supervised framework that addresses these requirements by constraining restoration operations and the training objective. Monotonic Chromatic Transform (MCT) restricts global color mapping in log-LMS space through positive diagonal entries and bounded cross-channel coefficients. Uncertainty-Gated Wavelet Reconstruction (UGWR) modulates high-frequency residuals with a learned confidence gate calibrated against normalized wavelet reconstruction errors. During training, Reference-Anchored Degradation Replay (RADR) renders the enhanced image and its paired reference using shared latent degradation proxies, encouraging both to reproduce the input. The three components share a restoration network that combines structured color correction, locally controlled detail recovery, and reference-anchored replay. MCL-UIE ranks first in thirteen of sixteen full-reference dataset-metric comparisons and produces competitive visual results on no-reference benchmarks. Objective ablations indicate that confidence calibration is most effective when combined with replay supervision. RADR is used only during training and adds no inference-time computation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Wangtao-Bao/MCL-UIE.

</details>


### [79] [COGS: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Attribute-Aware Object-Level 3D Gaussian Splatting Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39201)
*Zheng Xiao,Yifan Liu,Weijian Deng,Jiashu Li,Ziqin Liu,Zhenjun Han,Jianbin Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COGS大规模3DGS数据集（15,675个经伪影过滤、外观丰富的物体，40类）及COGS-Bench基准，系统诊断3D感知架构对异构高斯属性的利用方式，并提出保留属性角色信息的参考模型HALO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS越来越多被用作3D感知的输入表示，但现有架构如何利用外观、不透明度、协方差等异质高斯属性并不清楚，导致精度难以解释、难以进行有原则的架构设计；而现有物体级3DGS数据集缺乏外观多样性与伪影控制，无法支撑属性级系统分析。

Method: 构建COGS数据集（40类、15,675个伪影过滤且外观丰富的3DGS物体）；在其上建立COGS-Bench，用于物体分类与属性使用诊断，并结合外部数据集验证；进一步提出HALO参考模型，在层级聚合之前保留属性专属证据。

Result: 高斯属性并非一致有益：属性子集可优于完整属性集；外观、不透明度及协方差相关属性组合的效果在不同架构间差异显著；HALO在COGS-Bench与外部3DGS数据集上验证了角色保持式属性组织的价值。

Conclusion: COGS可支撑对3DGS属性使用方式的系统诊断，HALO表明保留属性角色信息的组织方式具有明确收益。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is increasingly explored as an input representation for 3D perception. Yet it remains unclear how current 3D architectures exploit heterogeneous Gaussian attributes. This ambiguity makes accuracy hard to interpret and limits principled architecture design for 3DGS perception. However, existing object-level 3DGS datasets lack the appearance variation or artifact control needed for systematic attribute-level analysis. We introduce COGS, a large-scale 3DGS dataset with 15,675 artifact-filtered and appearance-rich objects across 40 categories. Built on COGS, we establish COGS-Bench for object classification and attribute-usage diagnosis, complemented by external validation. Our benchmark shows that Gaussian attributes are not uniformly beneficial: selected subsets can outperform the full attribute set, and the effects of appearance, opacity, and covariance-related attribute combinations vary substantially across architectures. We further study this finding with HALO, a reference model that preserves attribute-specific evidence before hierarchical aggregation. Experiments on COGS-Bench and external 3DGS datasets demonstrate that COGS supports systematic diagnosis of 3DGS attribute usage, while HALO highlights the value of role-preserving attribute organization. Our dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/XiaoZhengCV/COGS.

</details>


### [80] [Seeing and Imagining as One: Unified Video Generation and 4D Perception for Embodied World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39232)
*Haitang Feng,Zhuoman Liu,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Juelin Li,Chengzhi Zhao,Hexiang Wei,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenSight将未来视频生成与稠密感知统一于单个预训练视频扩散Transformer中，实现"看见与想象"一体化的具身世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型不仅要想象合理的未来，还需感知演化世界中的几何、语义与运动结构；而现有方法通常采用分离的模态专用流水线，割裂了外观、几何、语义与运动的耦合。

Method: 基于共享DiT骨干，采用统一的时空表示编码视频、深度、具身分割与首帧相对2D光流；以轻量任务调制在同一架构内预测所有模态，并在统一的velocity prediction空间中联合优化；推理时支持两种互补模式：多步未来视频生成+对齐稠密预测，以及基于观测视频的单步稠密感知。

Result: 在涵盖多种机器人具身与人类手部交互的操作数据上训练，GenSight在视频生成与稠密4D感知两方面均表现优异，展现出跨模态一致性与生成先验对感知的增益。

Conclusion: 通过统一模态表示、模型架构与预测空间，GenSight避免了模态专用流水线，将外观、几何、语义与运动耦合于共享表示，验证了统一"看见与想象"对具身世界建模的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied world models should not only imagine plausible futures, but also perceive the geometric, semantic, and motion structure of the evolving world. We present GenSight, a unified embodied world model that brings future video generation and dense perception into a single pretrained video diffusion transformer. GenSight uses a unified spatiotemporal representation for video, depth, embodiment segmentation, and first-frame-relative 2D flow. Built on a shared DiT backbone, GenSight uses lightweight task modulation to predict all modalities within the same architecture. All tasks are jointly optimized within a unified velocity prediction space. At inference, GenSight supports two complementary modes: multi-step future video generation with aligned dense predictions and single-step dense perception from an observed video. By unifying modality representation, model architecture, and prediction space, GenSight avoids separate modality-specific pipelines and couples appearance, geometry, semantics, and motion in a shared representation. It promotes cross-modal consistency and allows dense perception to benefit from the spatiotemporal priors learned through video generation. Trained on embodied manipulation data spanning multiple robot embodiments and human-hand interactions, GenSight demonstrates strong performance in both video generation and dense 4D perception, highlighting the effectiveness of unifying seeing and imagining for embodied world modeling.

</details>


### [81] [DeCoRL: Decomposed Consistency Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Image Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39265)
*Zhiqiang Wu,Shuang Sun,Jiale Zhang,Jing Li,Yibo Chen,Xu Tang,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出强化学习框架DeCoRL，将整体多图合成一致性分解为对象级一致性以提升一致性，并构建DeCo-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图合成（MICo）中，随参考图数量增加，一致性迅速下降；SFT仅对合成图像提供隐式监督，难以学到可靠的对象级跨参考图对应关系。

Method: DeCoRL：先建立参考图间与指令相关的对象对应关系，并在生成图中定位对应区域；再用基于单对象一致性标注训练的VLM奖励模型给出细粒度对象级反馈，聚合成多对象一致性奖励用于RL后训练。

Result: 构建覆盖2至6张参考图的DeCo-Bench基准；在DeCo-Bench与公开MICo-Bench上均取得一致性的一致提升。

Conclusion: 将整体一致性显式分解为对象级一致性并借助细粒度奖励引导RL后训练，可有效改善多图合成一致性，具备良好扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-image composition (MICo) is a critical yet challenging capability for large-scale image editing models, where consistency degrades rapidly as the number of reference images increases. While supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can equip models with basic MICo ability, it provides only implicit supervision on the composed image and often fails to learn reliable object-level correspondence across multiple references. We propose DeCoRL, a reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that improves MICo consistency by decomposing holistic MICo consistency into object-level consistency. DeCoRL first establishes instruction-relevant object correspondences across reference images and localizes the corresponding regions in the generated image. It then uses a VLM-based reward model trained on single-object consistency annotations to produce fine-grained, object-wise feedback, which is aggregated into a multi-object consistency reward to guide RL post-training. We further introduce DeCo-Bench, a benchmark covering compositions from 2 to 6 reference images. Experiments on DeCo-Bench and the public MICo-Bench demonstrate consistent improvements in MICo consistency.

</details>


### [82] [Efficient Covariance-Aware Vicinal Latent Diffusion for Continuous Conditional Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39277)
*Xin Ding,Shuo Huang,Zhenglong Ding,Sen Zhang,Nenglun Chen,Peibei Cao,Yongwei Wang,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAViLD：面向连续条件生成的高效潜空间扩散框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续扩散模型在不平衡、长尾标签分布下存在严重数据稀疏，导致视觉伪影与条件不一致；同时推理阶段的classifier-free guidance与稠密像素空间积分带来巨大采样开销。

Method: 构建"净化"的条件感知潜空间：密度自适应邻域学习（density-adaptive vicinal learning）、辅助语义正则化、NIQE引导的负样本数据增强；用EMA教师内化条件引导方向以实现单分支条件推理；提出协方差感知的渐进蒸馏（covariance-aware progressive distillation）处理标签相关的概率流ODE。

Result: 在RC-49、UTKFace、Steering Angle上实现生成保真度、连续条件一致性与采样效率的良好权衡，采样可降至4 NFEs。

Conclusion: CAViLD通过条件感知潜空间净化与引导内化+渐进蒸馏，缓解长尾稀疏问题并大幅降低采样开销，适用于高效连续条件生成部署。

Abstract: Continuous Conditional Generative Modeling (CCGM) aims to synthesize high-dimensional data conditioned on continuously valued scalar labels. However, existing continuous diffusion models face severe data sparsity under imbalanced and long-tailed label distributions, which can lead to visual artifacts and condition inconsistency. Moreover, inference-time classifier-free guidance and dense pixel-space integration introduce substantial sampling overhead, limiting practical deployment. To address these challenges, we propose CAViLD, an efficient latent diffusion framework for continuous conditional generation. CAViLD first constructs a purified condition-aware latent space through density-adaptive vicinal learning, auxiliary semantic regularization, and NIQE-guided negative data augmentation. It then internalizes the conditional guidance direction through an EMA teacher, enabling single-branch conditional inference, and introduces covariance-aware progressive distillation for label-dependent probability-flow ODEs, supporting sampling down to four NFEs. Experiments on RC-49, UTKFace, and Steering Angle demonstrate a favorable trade-off among generation fidelity, continuous condition consistency, and sampling efficiency.

</details>


### [83] [WorldFlow: Joint Video and 3D Motion Prediction via Large-Scale Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39278)
*Yuhao Lu,Jiongxu Chen,Hexiang Wei,Jiajin Wu,Juelin Li,Mingjian Liang,Fan Yang,Zhuowei Liang,Zihao Liu,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出世界模型WorldFlow，联合预测未来视频与基于查询的3D轨迹，用共享MoT骨干在rectified-flow目标下统一去噪，实现显式几何动力学与视觉演化的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型将3D动力学隐含化，基于动作的公式绑定特定机器人构型，3D运动预测模型仅用光流监督、学习信号有限，难以支撑物理动力学建模。

Method: 将3D运动参数化为与视频网格对齐的连续位移token，采用统一Mixture-of-Transformers骨干，在rectified-flow目标下联合去噪视频与轨迹两种模态；在144万对象级样本（仿真+真实）混合数据上训练，并提出WorldFlowBench（11个数据源、1678样本，含空间推理任务）。

Result: 三组评测集上ADE与FDE均为学习类方法最低；SpatialCog上ADE为0.060 m，较最强外部基线降低54.2%；相较仅光流变体，ADE与FDE分别降低55.9%和68.5%。

Conclusion: 显式3D位移token与视频联合生成的统一架构可有效释放预训练视频模型中的物理常识，在精度与泛化上优于隐式动力学与纯光流监督方案。

Abstract: Predicting future physical states stands as a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing video world models leave 3D dynamics implicit, action-based formulations remain tied to specific robot configurations, and 3D motion prediction models use flow-only supervision, providing a limited learning signal for physical dynamics. To circumvent these limitations, we introduce \method{}, a generative world model that jointly predicts future video and query-based 3D trajectories. By parameterizing 3D motion as continuous displacement tokens aligned with the video grid, a unified Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone jointly denoises both modalities under a rectified-flow objective. This formulation couples explicit geometric dynamics with visual evolution, effectively unlocking the physical commonsense embedded within pretrained video models. To empirically scale and validate this unified architecture, we train \method{} on a heterogeneous mixture of 1.44 million object-level samples spanning simulated and real-world domains. We further introduce WorldFlowBench, comprising 1,678 evaluation samples from 11 data sources across simulated and real-world interactions, including spatial reasoning tasks. Across all three evaluation sets, WorldFlow achieves the lowest average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) among the evaluated learning-based methods. Notably, on the challenging SpatialCog set, WorldFlow achieves an ADE of 0.060 m, a 54.2\% reduction compared with the strongest external baseline. It also reduces ADE and FDE by 55.9\% and 68.5\%, respectively, compared with its flow-only variant. Project page: https://worldflow-rbs.github.io/.

</details>


### [84] [PriorMaskMap: Robust Online Vectorized Map Construction with Biased Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39283)
*Haoming Xu,Wei Li,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PriorMaskMap：一种面向先验不完美场景的鲁棒先验引导矢量化地图构建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线矢量化地图构建需实时语义几何理解，先验地图可提升精度，但先验常因信息过时或道路临时变化而产生偏差；现有方法将先验视为完全可靠或仅考虑有限噪声模式，在先验不完美时性能严重下降。

Method: 提出 PriorMaskMap，含三项设计：Prior-Aware Confidence Estimator（PACE）将输入栅格化地图与当前视觉观测比对，为可靠/错误/空白区域分配置信度分数，生成基础掩码并引导两条分支；Dual-Prior Query Generator（DPQG）选择性过滤并嵌入栅格与矢量先验，从可信地图结构初始化信息量高的查询；Confidence-Guided Prior Fusion（CGPF）在空间上传播并细化掩码，以细化后的掩码控制 BEV 特征与先验地图的融合，为地图解码器提供丰富上下文表示。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上，PriorMaskMap 的地图精度优于现有方法，并在先验含明显错误时表现出显著鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式估计先验置信度并据此引导查询生成与特征融合，可有效缓解不完美先验带来的性能退化，实现更精确、更鲁棒的矢量化地图构建。

Abstract: Online vectorized map construction provides autonomous vehicles with essential, real-time semantic and geometric scene understanding. While leveraging prior maps presents an opportunity to improve accuracy, they are often biased due to outdated information or temporary road changes. Existing methods, which typically treat priors as fully reliable or only consider limited noise patterns, suffer severe performance degradation when these priors are imperfect. To address this, we propose PriorMaskMap, a robust framework for prior-guided vectorized map construction. Its first innovation is a Prior-Aware Confidence Estimator (PACE) that evaluates the input rasterized map against current visual observations. It assigns confidence scores to regions classified as reliable, erroneous, or blank, creating a foundational mask that guides two subsequent branches. In one branch, the Dual-Prior Query Generator (DPQG) selectively filters and embeds both rasterized and vectorized priors to initialize informative queries from trustworthy map structures. In the other, the Confidence-Guided Prior Fusion (CGPF) module spatially propagates and refines the mask, using the refined version to govern the fusion of BEV features with the prior map, thereby providing rich contextual representations for the map decoder. Evaluation on the nuScenes dataset shows that PriorMaskMap achieves superior mapping precision over existing approaches and exhibits marked robustness when provided with priors containing apparent errors. Our code is released at https://github.com/healenrens/PriorMaskMap.

</details>


### [85] [Unlocking Any-Order Generation in Pretrained Autoregressive Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39293)
*Rishav Pramanik,Marco Pedersoli,Zhaozheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将预训练光栅扫描自回归（AR）图像生成器适配为任意顺序生成器的配方，仅用约13-33%额外算力即可获得修复、外扩与编辑能力，且不改变标准AR推理栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定光栅扫描顺序把单一因子分解硬编码进模型，导致预训练AR模型无法在推理时做修复、外扩或编辑；现有离散扩散类方案需从头在多种token顺序混合上训练，算力约为光栅扫描的3倍，且牺牲KV缓存、vLLM等AR推理生态兼容性。作者认为这一代价不必要：预训练模型的缺陷不在于缺乏知识，而在于缺乏通过非顺序条件路径表达已知联合分布的能力。

Method: 两个组件：（1）future-aware positional embeddings，以可忽略开销解决目标位置可辨识性问题；（2）ordering curriculum，引导从预训练解稳定过渡到良好的任意顺序收敛域。标准AR推理栈保持不变。

Result: 在两种架构、两种规模共四个基座（LlamaGen-L/XL、RAR-L/XL）上：ImageNet修复FID 6.21 vs. MaskGIT 10.44，外扩亦显著更优；与MAR-L相当；类别条件FID仅增加1.2-1.9；额外算力为预训练的13-33%。

Conclusion: 修复、外扩与编辑能力无需作为新模块加入AR图像模型，可从既有预训练模型中"解锁"，从而实现灵活生成与AR推理效率的兼得。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) image generators are powerful in principle but inflexible in practice: training with a fixed raster-scan order hardcodes a single factorization of the image distribution into the model, leaving it unable to inpaint, outpaint, or edit at inference time. Existing solutions, including the modern discrete-diffusion family, train from scratch on a mixture of token orderings, paying roughly 3x the compute of standard raster-scan training; discrete diffusion further sacrifices compatibility with the AR inference ecosystem (KV caching, vLLM) by construction. We argue that this cost is unnecessary. A pretrained raster-scan AR model has already learned the joint distribution over image tokens; what it lacks is not knowledge but the ability to express that knowledge through non-sequential conditional pathways. We propose a recipe for adapting pretrained raster-scan models into any-order generators using two components: future-aware positional embeddings that resolve a target-position identifiability problem at negligible overhead, and an ordering curriculum that traces a stable optimization path from the pretrained solution to a good any-order basin. Applied to four base models across two architectures and two scales (LlamaGen-L/XL, RAR-L/XL), the recipe substantially outperforms MaskGIT on ImageNet inpainting (FID 6.21 vs. 10.44) and outpainting, is competitive with MAR-L, trades a 1.2-1.9 increase in class-conditional FID for this flexibility, and costs 13-33% additional compute beyond pretraining, while leaving the standard AR inference stack untouched. Inpainting, outpainting, and editing, our results suggest, need not be added to autoregressive image models. They can be unlocked from the ones that exist. Code is available at https://github.com/Ordered-AR/unlockedadaptation.

</details>


### [86] [Youtu-Parsing-Omni: One Encoder, One Schema, All Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39301)
*Yunhang Shen,Zhangquan Chen,Xiaotian Li,Zhongpeng Cai,Qing Zhou,Zekai Zhang,Kun Zhang,Zezhong Wu,Jian Hu,Haihan Gao,Heting Gao,Xin Li,Yinsong Liu,Deqian Jiang,Di Yin,Haoyu Cao,Xing Sun,Bing Liu,Yanqiu Qu,Yunfei Wu,Kun Yin,Zhixiang Wei,Yongxiang Hua,Qianyu Li,Xiaoxing Hu,Peiyuan Zhang,Lijiang Li,Dong Xu,Zhenchuan Yang,Yiliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全模态解析器 Youtu-Parsing-Omni：单一编码器 + 单一模式，将文档/图像/音频/视频转为时空定位的结构化记录，5B 参数单检查点即取得多项 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为智能体提供跨模态的感知层，使其能读取、定位并引用证据；现有解析系统模态割裂、任务输出不统一，难以支撑跨模态定位与引用。

Method: 1) Youtu-Omni-Encoder：由预训练语言模型初始化以强化文本识别，共享权重在统一时空坐标系中编码所有模态；少量融合层让视频帧关注同时刻音频，把跨模态融合与时间对齐内置于感知阶段。2) OmniSchema：统一十个解析任务的输出形式。3) OSAD（OmniSchema-Aware On-Policy Distillation）：基于共享 token-role 目标的单阶段自演化后训练，每轮用模型自身 rollout 对参考条件化副本的 token 级目标训练，再刷新该副本，实现解析器与监督共同演化。

Result: 单一 5B 检查点在 OmniDocBench v1.6 达 96.96（Overall，SOTA）；OmniParsingBench 75.08（Avg.），领先所有开放权重模型，优势集中于音频与视频；图表解析总分最佳（95.02）；化学指纹精确匹配率最高；排版乐谱字符级转写与专用光学音乐模型相当，显著优于通用系统。

Conclusion: 统一编码器、统一 schema 与自演化后训练可在单一中等规模模型中实现跨模态解析 SOTA，验证了"感知内置融合"路线的有效性；代码与权重已公开。

Abstract: We present Youtu-Parsing-Omni, an omni-modal parser that converts documents, images, audio, and video into structured records grounded in space and time, providing a perception layer for agents that read, locate, and cite evidence across modalities. Its design rests on one encoder for every modality and one schema for every task. Youtu-Omni-Encoder, initialized from a pre-trained language model to strengthen text reading, encodes all modalities with shared weights in a unified space-time coordinate system, and a few fusion layers let video frames attend to concurrent audio, bringing cross-modal fusion and temporal alignment into perception itself. OmniSchema unifies the outputs of all ten parsing tasks, allowing a single self-evolving post-training procedure, OmniSchema-Aware On-Policy Distillation (OSAD), to provide a shared token-role-based objective across tasks. In each round, the model is trained on its own rollouts against token-level targets from a reference-conditioned copy of itself, which is then refreshed with the trained weights, so that the parser and its supervision co-evolve. With a single 5 B-parameter checkpoint, Youtu-Parsing-Omni achieves state-of-the-art performance on OmniDocBench v1.6 (96.96 Overall) and leads all open-weight models on OmniParsingBench (75.08 Avg.), with its advantage concentrated on audio and video. It also achieves the best overall chart-parsing score (95.02) and the highest exact chemical-fingerprint agreement among compared systems, and matches a dedicated optical-music model in character-level transcription of typeset sheet music while staying far ahead of general-purpose systems. Code and model weights are publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [CAD-WAM: Anticipating Geometry for Text-to-CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39306)
*Zhengyang Li,Weijie Zheng,Yuxin Cui,Sijie Wang,Wenjie Feng,Qiyu Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAD-WAM 借鉴 world-action model 思想，将几何条件动作生成与下一状态预测耦合，用 LLM 主干同时预测 CAD DSL 操作和中间实体的几何状态，从文本生成可编辑 CAD 模型，在 DeepCAD 测试集上精度与有效性均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本生成可编辑 CAD 模型需逐步选择操作，单步错误会累积影响后续步骤；现有基于 LLM 的方法只学习预测命令，缺乏对命令应产生几何状态的显式监督，导致多步生成可靠性不足。

Method: 将完整建模操作定义为 CAD DSL 中的 action；用预训练 VAE 把中间实体的表面点云编码为紧凑隐状态；LLM 主干根据目标描述与当前几何状态生成下一 action，同一主干上的辅助头预测下一状态以提供训练期几何监督；由 DeepCAD 参数化模型构建分步轨迹并配专家级文本描述；推理时每个生成动作由 CAD kernel 执行，结果实体重新编码用于下一步决策。

Result: 在 DeepCAD 测试集上，几何精度与输出有效性优于任务专用基线，且在需要更多操作的模型上增益更大；相比使用同一 DSL 提示的通用 LLM 生成几何更准确；消融显示，与仅训练动作预测的相同模型相比，下一状态监督使平均 Chamfer 距离降低 13.2%。

Conclusion: 显式引入下一几何状态预测作为辅助监督，可有效提升 LLM 驱动的多步 CAD 生成可靠性与准确性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Generating editable computer-aided design (CAD) models from text requires selecting operations that produce the intended changes to the geometry constructed so far. An incorrect operation can compromise subsequent steps, making reliable multi-step generation challenging. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) primarily learn to predict commands, without explicit supervision of the geometric states those commands should produce. Inspired by world-action models, we introduce CAD-WAM, which couples geometry-conditioned action generation with next-state prediction. We define complete modeling operations as actions in a CAD domain-specific language (DSL) and encode surface point clouds of intermediate solids into compact latent states using a pretrained variational autoencoder. An LLM backbone generates the next action from the target description and current geometric state, while an auxiliary head on the same backbone predicts the next state to provide geometric supervision during training. We construct step-wise training trajectories from parametric CAD models in the DeepCAD dataset, pairing each trajectory with an expert-level textual description. At inference, each generated action is executed by a CAD kernel, and the resulting solid is re-encoded to inform the next decision. On the DeepCAD test set, CAD-WAM outperforms the task-specific baselines in geometric accuracy and output validity, with larger gains on models requiring more operations, and produces more accurate geometry than general-purpose LLMs prompted with the same DSL. Ablations further show that next-state supervision reduces mean Chamfer distance by 13.2\% relative to an otherwise identical model trained only to predict actions. Code is available at https://github.com/Lzy-1037/CAD-WAM.

</details>


### [88] [MemoryFusion: Cross-Temporal Memory Learning for Multimodal Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39322)
*Meiqi Gong,Hao Zhang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架 MemoryFusion，通过跨时序记忆学习与渐进式时序精炼，实现多模态视频融合中的长时信息利用与时序一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频融合方法主要依赖短程时序交互，长期信息利用不足；同时融合表示不一致会导致闪烁伪影、破坏时序连贯性。这两类问题相互耦合，需要统一解决。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段：结合短期记忆库（局部时序引导）与长期记忆库（面向融合的更新策略保存可靠历史信息）；并用时序平滑模块借助软运动线索进行粗粒度背景稳定。第二阶段：在融合先验基础上，使用轻量级 3D 残差时序精炼模块修正残余时序不一致并增强结构细节。

Result: 大量实验表明，MemoryFusion 在融合质量与时序一致性上均优于现有基于帧和基于视频的融合方法。

Conclusion: 长短时记忆结合与渐进式时序精炼能够有效缓解闪烁伪影并提升视频融合的时序连贯性，源码已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal videos contain rich temporal cues that can facilitate faithful fusion. However, existing video fusion methods mainly rely on short-range temporal interactions, leaving valuable long-term information underexplored. Moreover, inconsistent fusion representations can cause flickering artifacts, undermining temporal coherence. To address these coupled challenges, we propose MemoryFusion, a two-stage framework integrating cross-temporal memory learning with progressive temporal refinement. Considering the complementary roles of adjacent and distant frames, the first stage combines a Short-Term Memory Bank for local temporal guidance with a Long-Term Memory Bank that preserves reliable historical information through a fusion-oriented update strategy. To mitigate temporal fluctuations, a Temporal Smoothing Module leverages soft motion cues for coarse background stabilization. Building upon the resulting fusion prior, The second stage further employs a lightweight 3D residual Temporal Refinement Module to correct remaining temporal inconsistencies while enhancing structural details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MemoryFusion achieves superior fusion quality and temporal consistency over existing frame-based and video-based fusion methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Meiqi-Gong/MemoryFusion.

</details>


### [89] [MRC-MMAgent: Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Role Collaboration in Multimodal Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39324)
*Shengqin Wang,Jie Jin,Xiaofan Li,Wentao Yan,Yu Cheng,Qingsong HuYihang Chen,Weilin Luo,Jingyu Gong,Xin Tan,Yuan Xie,Zhizhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多角色协同强化学习框架 MRC-MMAgent，将轨迹评估与续接嵌入可执行智能体回路，优化搜索—审查—修正的全轨迹过程，在多个基准上取得领先或极具竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态工具智能体强化学习多为单角色、基于终局奖励训练搜索与作答，忽视人类式“检查证据—重构查询—修正结论”的自我纠错过程，因而无法在完整交互轨迹上优化该过程。

Method: 构建融合求解、审查与续接的多角色协同回路：智能体通过工具获取证据、评估前序轨迹，并在当前答案支撑不足时恢复解题；设计角色耦合学习目标，将任务成功、交互有效性与成功修正结合，并对所有角色决策进行全轨迹策略优化。

Result: 仅使用原始基线训练集、不额外构建数据集，方法稳定优于对应基座模型，并在各基准上达到领先或高度竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 将自纠错能力显式建模为可执行的多角色协同回路并做全轨迹策略优化，是提升多模态工具智能体的有效途径，且无需额外数据即可泛化。

Abstract: Multimodal tool-using agents augment vision-language models with access to external information and capabilities, enabling them to solve knowledge-intensive and visually complex tasks that cannot be addressed using visual context alone. However, existing reinforcement learning pipelines largely train single-role agents to search and answer using terminal rewards, overlooking how humans inspect retrieved evidence, reformulate queries, and revise conclusions during search. Consequently, they fail to optimize this self-corrective process over complete interaction trajectories. We propose MRC-MMAgent, a multi-role collaborative reinforcement learning framework that incorporates trajectory assessment and continuation directly into the executable agent loop. First, the framework establishes a multi-role collaborative loop that integrates solving, inspection, and continuation, enabling the agent to acquire evidence through tools, evaluate the preceding trajectory, and resume problem solving when the current answer is insufficiently supported. Second, we introduce a role-coupled learning objective that combines task success, interaction validity, and successful correction with full-trajectory policy optimization across the decisions generated by all roles. Extensive experiments show that, using only the original baseline training set without constructing additional datasets, our method consistently outperforms the corresponding base models and achieves leading or highly competitive performance across benchmarks.

</details>


### [90] [Can Image Agents Complete Visual Commissions? A Benchmark and Modular Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39330)
*Tong Li,Lishuai Gao,Qi Zeng,Yifan Li,Cong Wei,Zhuoliang Kang,Xiaoming Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉委派基准VCB与参考智能体V-Protocol，通过推理时动态组合任务、认知、表达三类可复用模块，在统一控制器与图像生成器下将严格任务成功率提升至69.0%（基线SCOPE为42.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉委派（visual commission）要求图像智能体整合异构素材、在多级依赖阶段中保持需求一致并产出协调的交付物。现有评测多聚焦单一能力与特定执行设置，端到端委派完成能力缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 构建VCB基准：600个任务、12个类别、三个互补层级（从能力导向任务到完整委派），采用5,039项需求检查与独立的视觉质量评分，评估内容正确性、视觉实现、约束保持与交付完整性，且不规定内部工作流。提出V-Protocol参考智能体：推理时动态组合可复用的任务模块（组织交付）、认知模块（推导内容）、表达模块（引导视觉实现）；模块间交换结构化中间产物，共享执行框架追踪产物版本与依赖、管理工具调用并校验完成度。

Result: 在与智能体基线相同的控制器和图像生成器条件下，V-Protocol在VCB上取得69.0%严格任务成功率（SCOPE为42.0%）；在Mind-Bench上严格准确率由39.2%提升至45.4%。

Conclusion: 模块化动态组合架构能有效提升端到端视觉委派的严格完成度与跨基准泛化性，且评测设计应覆盖完整交付链路而非孤立能力。

Abstract: Visual commissions require image agents to integrate heterogeneous source materials, preserve requirements across dependent stages, and produce coordinated deliverables. Existing evaluations examine specific capabilities and execution settings, leaving end-to-end commission completion less understood. We introduce Visual Commission Bench (VCB), comprising 600 tasks across 12 categories and three complementary tiers, from capability-focused tasks to complete commissions. VCB uses 5,039 requirement checks and separate visual quality ratings to assess content correctness, visual realization, constraint preservation, and delivery completeness without prescribing an internal workflow. We present V-Protocol, a reference agent that dynamically composes reusable task, cognitive, and expression modules at inference time. Task modules organize delivery, cognitive modules derive content, and expression modules guide visual realization. The modules exchange structured intermediate artifacts, while a shared execution harness tracks artifact revisions and dependencies, manages tool execution, and checks completion. With the same controller and image generator as the agent baselines, V-Protocol achieves 69.0\% strict task success on VCB, compared with 42.0\% for SCOPE. On Mind-Bench, strict accuracy increases from 39.2\% to 45.4\%.

</details>


### [91] [Telling Is Not Seeing: Textual Personas Rarely Make Vision-Language Models Answer Like the People They Describe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39331)
*Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提示词中的"人格设定"（如"你有低视力"）并不能让视觉语言模型"看见"所描述人群的视觉体验；模型只是复述它已知的人群知识，其表现不及仅使用人格陈述事实的简单规则，并抹平了群体与个体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户研究常以人格提示让VLM"扮演"特定人群（低视力、色盲、听力损失者）来替代真实被试。作者质疑这种做法是否成立：人格设定是否真能让模型的视觉感知接近所描述的人？

Method: 将人格提示下模型的回答与三类基准比较：(1) 真实测量的低视力、色盲、听力损失人群数据；(2) 仅使用人格陈述事实的简单规则。测试14个模型；对比"用文字描述模糊"与"对图像施加模糊"两种条件；并分析开放模型中人格描述是否改变图像内部表征与答案概率分布；最后测试模型对模糊与视错觉影响人类的估计能力。

Result: 人格设定的表现从未超过简单规则；它抹除了群体间与个体间的真实差异。在全部14个模型中，文字描述"模糊"并不改变识别结果，而实际模糊图像则如对真人一样降低识别表现。在开放模型内部，人格描述既不改变图像信息，也不改变答案概率。然而模型能正确估计模糊与错觉对人的影响。

Conclusion: "说"不等于"看"：人格提示让模型说出它关于人的知识，而非让它看见人所看见的。以人格提示替代真实用户研究存在效度问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly prompted with personas, such as "You have low vision", to stand in for people in user studies. We ask whether a persona makes a model see like the person it describes. We compare persona answers with measured people with low vision, color blindness or hearing loss, and with simple rules that use only the facts the persona states. The persona never beats these rules, and it erases the real differences between groups and individuals. Describing blur leaves recognition unchanged in all 14 models we test, while blurring the photo lowers it as it does for people. Inside open models, the description changes neither the image information nor the answer probabilities. Yet the models know the effects they fail to show: they can estimate how blur and illusions affect people. Telling is not seeing: a persona makes a model say what it knows about people, not see what they see.

</details>


### [92] [PicturePerfect: Diffusion-based Same-identity Facial Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39344)
*Manik Dhingra,Yashesh Savani,Mahesh Reddy,Antoine Mercier,Jiahe Liu,Guillaume Berger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预训练文本到图像扩散模型的同身份人脸替换方法：输入一张人像图和同一人的第二张参考图，在保持背景不变的前提下将输入人脸无缝替换为参考人脸，并构建了大规模同人不同表情/姿态的图像对数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在计算机视觉中成效显著，但"同身份人脸替换"这一特定设定（换脸但保留背景、参考图为同一人且条件不同）尽管有明确现实用途，近期却无工作直接研究，因此需要针对性方法。

Method: 在预训练文本到图像扩散模型基础上构建替换方法，以输入人像与同一人的参考图像为条件，实现身份一致的人脸替换并保持背景完整；同时构建了大规模同人、不同表情与姿态的图像对数据集以支撑该任务。

Result: 实验表明，该方法生成的结果在真实感和身份一致性上优于现有的人脸重演（face reenactment）模型。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可有效用于同身份人脸替换，在该特定设定下相较现有重演方法具有优势；配套数据集的构建也为该任务提供了支撑。

Abstract: With the growing success of diffusion models in computer vision, we explore their potential for same-identity facial replacement in photographs. Specifically, we propose a diffusion-based method, built on top of a pre-trained text-to-image model, that takes as input a portrait image of a person and a second reference image of the same individual, potentially captured under different conditions. The goal is to seamlessly replace the input face with the reference face, while keeping the background intact. Surprisingly, despite the clear real-world utility of this task, no recently published work has directly addressed face replacement in this specific setting. To support this goal, we construct a large dataset of image pairs depicting the same person under varying facial expressions and poses. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach produces more realistic and identity-consistent results than existing face reenactment models.

</details>


### [93] [Semantic Mismapping in Generalized Category Discovery: Evaluation and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39356)
*Zitai Wang,Qianqian Xu,Cong Hua,Xi Chen,Zhiyong Yang,Xiaochun Cao,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示广义类别发现（GCD）中全局匈牙利匹配导致的"语义错配"问题（可使新类准确率高估达25.63点），提出语义保持评估（SPE）与错配缓解优化（MMO）来修正评估与训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期GCD方法采用可训练参数化头，使输出空间部分可交换：旧类索引有语义锚定，新类索引仍可交换。但仍使用全局匈牙利匹配，会把已锚定的旧索引重新分配给其他类别，掩盖预测错误。作者观察到这种重分配可使新类准确率高估最高达25.63点，称之为语义错配，并推导出刻画其何时能提高匹配分数的置换环条件。

Method: 1) SPE：保留旧类索引的监督语义，仅在新区间内进行匈牙利匹配；2) MMO：结合区域平衡损失（RBL，用于旧/新路由）与新类多样性损失（NDL，鼓励新原型正交）来缓解引发语义错配的预测错误；3) 理论分析：证明最小化这两个损失可收紧语义错配带来的虚假准确率增益上界，且残差项可控。

Result: 语义错配导致新类准确率高估最高25.63点；在GH与SPE两种评估下，所提方法均显示出有效性。

Conclusion: 全局匹配在部分可交换输出空间中存在评估缺陷，SPE通过限制匹配范围保持语义正确性，MMO通过两项损失在理论上收紧虚假增益上界；实验在GH与SPE下验证了方法有效性。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to perform old-category recognition and novel-category discovery in the same unlabeled set. Early methods address both objectives through clustering and evaluate the exchangeable predicted indices with global Hungarian (GH) matching. Recent methods adopt trainable parametric heads, using supervision for old-category recognition and discovery-oriented objectives for new categories. In this way, the output space becomes partially exchangeable: old indices are semantically anchored, while new indices remain exchangeable. However, these methods still apply global matching, which can reassign anchored old indices to other categories and hide some prediction errors. Empirically, we are surprised to observe that this reassignment can induce an overestimation of new-category accuracy by up to 25.63 points. We term this phenomenon semantic mismapping and further derive a permutation-cycle condition that characterizes when semantic mismapping can increase the global matching score. To fix this issue, we propose Semantics-Preserving Evaluation (SPE), which preserves the supervised semantics of old indices and restricts Hungarian matching to the new region. Furthermore, we propose Mismapping-Mitigating Optimization (MMO) to alleviate the prediction errors that can give rise to semantic mismapping, combining a region-balance loss (RBL) for old/new routing with a novel-diversity loss (NDL) that encourages orthogonality among new prototypes. Theoretically, we show that minimizing the two losses can tighten an upper bound on spurious accuracy gains from semantic mismapping, with controllable residual terms. Empirical results under both GH and SPE demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>


### [94] [CASE: Concept-Aware Segmentation from Positive and Negative Visual Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39396)
*Yi-Jen Tsai,Yen-Yu Lin,Chien-Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"指定视觉提示分割"(SVPS)新设定：目标概念由正、负视觉提示共同定义，并构建CASE基准（基于COCO/PACO，含六类类别/属性级任务），发现冻结SAM 3不能稳定将负提示作为排除证据，进而提出保守的LoRA适配基线CPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本语义分割通常只依赖单一语义类别的正样本支持，正线索往往欠定义：如"red cars"会混淆所有颜色的车，"vehicles"会包含应排除的car，材料定义的对象会跨多类别。精确请求需要正样本提供包含证据、负样本提供排除与消歧的意图约束。

Method: 将设定形式化为SVPS（视觉样例指由边界框提示指定的支持图像区域）；构建CASE基准（COCO+PACO），涵盖exclusion、isolation、union、attribute exclusion、attribute isolation、attribute union六类任务族；在该设定下评估SAM 3；提出Concept Prompt Adaptation (CPA)——基于LoRA的保守适配基线，以改进正负提示的联合解读。

Result: 冻结SAM 3虽具备强正样本少样本分割能力，但不能一致地将负视觉提示视为排除证据；CPA在CASE上提升正负提示解读效果，同时在纯正样本少样本分割下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 正负视觉提示联合指定是更实用的分割范式，现有强模型在此存在系统性缺陷；保守LoRA适配可在不牺牲原能力的前提下部分弥补，SVPS与CASE为后续研究提供问题定义与评测平台。

Abstract: Few-shot semantic segmentation typically identifies target objects based on positive support examples of a single semantic class. In practice, however, positive cues alone are often underspecified: for instance, "red cars" may be confused with cars of all colors, "vehicles" may include cars that should be excluded, and "material-defined objects" may span multiple categories. Fulfilling precise requests requires positive exemplars to provide inclusion evidence and negative exemplars to act as intent-defining constraints for exclusion and disambiguation. We formulate this setting as Specified Visual Prompt Segmentation (SVPS), where the target concept is specified by both positive and negative visual prompts. In this work, visual exemplars refer to support-image regions specified by bounding-box prompts. To study SVPS, we introduce CASE (Concept-Aware Segmentation from Positive and Negative Visual Exemplars), a benchmark built on COCO and PACO. CASE encompasses six category- and attribute-level task families: exclusion, isolation, union, attribute exclusion, attribute isolation, and attribute union. We evaluate SAM 3 under this setting and find that, despite its powerful positive-only few-shot segmentation capability, frozen SAM 3 does not consistently treat negative visual prompts as exclusion evidence. To address this, we present Concept Prompt Adaptation (CPA), a conservative LoRA-based adaptation baseline that improves positive-negative prompt interpretation on CASE while remaining competitive under positive-only few-shot segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/WongKinYiu/FSS-CASE.

</details>


### [95] [ACD-GS: Asymmetric Curvature-aware Densification for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39399)
*Houqiang Zhong,Tianchi Zhu,Qiang Hu,Xiaoyun Zhang,Li Song,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACD-GS提出基于二阶深度响应的非对称曲率感知稠密化框架，在训练中直接抑制冗余高斯生成，高斯数量与存储减少约60%仍保持有竞争力的渲染保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS训练期稠密化高度启发式，仅依赖一阶屏幕空间梯度触发克隆与分裂，无法区分真实几何不连续与高频纹理或噪声残差，导致高斯冗余增殖与几何重建不准确。

Method: 以二阶深度响应作为密度控制的结构先验，对克隆与分裂实施非对称调控；引入多视角光度一致性约束抑制单视角瞬态误差；并采用容量感知自适应调节策略平衡训练中的稠密化与剪枝。属训练期冗余抑制而非后处理压缩或量化。

Result: 高斯数量与存储减少近60%，渲染保真度保持竞争力，在多个数据集上稳定优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 深度二阶响应是更可靠的稠密化结构先验，可在训练中直接获得紧凑且几何感知的三维高斯表示。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for real-time photorealistic novel view synthesis. However, its training-time densification remains largely heuristic, often resulting in redundant Gaussian proliferation and inaccurate geometric reconstruction. Existing methods predominantly rely on first-order screen-space gradients to trigger cloning and splitting, which fail to distinguish true geometric discontinuities from high-frequency textures or noisy residuals. To address this limitation, we propose ACD-GS, an Asymmetric Curvature-aware Densification framework for compact and geometry-aware 3D Gaussian optimization. Our key insight is that second-order depth responses provide a more reliable structural prior for density control, enabling asymmetric regulation of cloning and splitting to better capture geometric discontinuities. Building on this, we introduce a multi-view photometric consistency constraint to suppress transient single-view errors, together with a capacity-aware adaptive regulation strategy that balances densification and pruning during training. Unlike post-hoc compression or quantization-based approaches, ACD-GS directly prevents redundant Gaussian generation during training, yielding inherently compact representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method reduces nearly 60% of Gaussian count and storage while maintaining competitive rendering fidelity, consistently outperforming existing methods across multiple datasets. Our code is available in https://waveviewer.github.io/ACD-GS/.

</details>


### [96] [SymKL: A Unified Representation-Space Measure of Signal Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39403)
*Dhananjaya Jayasundara,Sudarshan Rajagopalan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SymKL——基于隐式神经表示（INR）的统一全参考退化度量：对原始参考信号拟合一次INR，以其对角Fisher信息刻画表示的信息几何，用参考与退化观测分别评估Fisher得到两组参数敏感度分布，其对称KL散度即退化分数；并给出SIREN对角Fisher的闭式表达。实验表明其在图像、语音、体三维基准上单调跟踪退化强度，且跨模态通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全参考质量/退化度量本质上与模态绑定（如图像的PSNR/SSIM、音频的PESQ等），无法在图像、音频、3D数据间通用；且许多度量需要已知失真类型或强度先验，在退化严重时易饱和失效。作者希望构建一个仅依赖参考与退化观测、无需知道损坏过程的模态无关退化度量。

Method: 1) 对原始参考信号拟合一次INR（如SIREN），获得其参数化表示；2) 以对角Fisher信息刻画该表示在参数空间的局部信息几何（参数敏感度）；3) 分别在参考信号与退化观测上评估Fisher信息，得到两个参数敏感度剖面；4) 取两剖面的对称KL散度作为退化分数；5) 推导SIREN对角Fisher信息的精确闭式表达式以实现高效计算。方法只需参考与退化观测，不依赖损坏过程知识。

Result: 在图像、语音与体三维（volumetric 3D）基准上，SymKL能准确跟踪退化严重程度；在传统度量出现饱和或失效的区间仍保持单调性；并可作为跨模态统一的退化度量。代码将在评审后公开。

Conclusion: 将全参考退化度量从模态特定的手工特征转向基于INR参数空间信息几何的统一框架，SymKL提供了一种无需失真先验、可跨图像/音频/3D使用的退化量化方式，并借助SIREN的Fisher闭式解保持计算可行性。

Abstract: Quantifying degradation between a pristine signal and its degraded observation is fundamental to signal processing, yet existing full-reference measures are inherently modality-specific. We introduce SymKL, a unified full-reference degradation measure based on implicit neural representations (INRs). An INR is fitted once to the pristine reference and characterized by its diagonal Fisher information, which captures the local information geometry of the learned representation. Evaluating the Fisher information with the pristine reference and a degraded observation yields two parameter-sensitivity profiles whose symmetric KL divergence defines the degradation score. The proposed formulation requires only the reference and degraded observation, without knowledge of the corruption process or degradation severity, making it applicable across images, audio, and 3D data. We further derive an exact closed-form expression for the diagonal Fisher of SIREN, enabling efficient computation. Experiments on image, speech, and volumetric 3D benchmarks demonstrate that SymKL accurately tracks degradation severity, remains monotonic where conventional metrics saturate or fail, and provides a unified modality-agnostic measure of signal degradation. Code will be made publicly available after the review process.

</details>


### [97] [YOLOF-MLD: A Multi-Level Distillation Framework for Efficient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39408)
*Xiaolong Wu,Liming Zheng,Zitong Liu,Yue Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出YOLOF-MLD：以SiSo颈替换参数密集的FPN，并用多级蒸馏（MLD）补偿精度损失，在边缘CPU上实现轻量化火烟检测的高精度-低延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限边缘设备（如火烟监测等安全关键场景）需高效目标检测。现有轻量框架如YOLO26仍依赖参数密集的多分支FPN，无法充分发挥轻量优势；直接用YOLOF的单输入单输出（SiSo）颈替换FPN虽能减参，但会造成显著精度下降。

Method: 提出YOLOF-MLD，一个面向CPU的高效多级蒸馏（MLD）框架，由YOLO26n主干、SiSo颈和MLD组成。MLD在三个层级进行对齐：主干层采用切片语义匹配（sliced-semantics matching），颈部采用感受野匹配（receptive-field matching），以及响应层蒸馏。

Result: 在D-Fire与Annotated Fire-Smoke（AFS）数据集上，相较YOLO26n参数减少35.7%，在树莓派5上实现62.3%的加速；mAP@50分别为71.6%和59.9%，YOLO26n对应为75.5%和60.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法在边缘部署中具备有竞争力的精度-延迟权衡；代码已开源。

Abstract: Efficient object detection on resource-constrained edge devices is essential for safety-critical applications such as fire-smoke monitoring. However, current lightweight frameworks, such as YOLO26, still rely on the parameter-dense multi-branch Feature Pyramid Network (FPN), preventing full exploitation of their lightweight advantage. Replacing FPN with YOLOF's single-input-single-output (SiSo) neck is a promising solution but causes substantial accuracy loss. To alleviate this, we propose YOLOF-MLD, a CPU-efficient multi-level distillation (MLD) framework that combines a YOLO26n backbone, a SiSo neck, and MLD. It aligns features at the backbone via sliced-semantics matching, neck with receptive-field matching, and response levels. On D-Fire and Annotated Fire-Smoke (AFS) datasets, YOLOF-MLD reduces the parameter count of YOLO26n by 35.7\% and achieves a 62.3\% speedup on Raspberry Pi 5, attaining 71.6% and 59.9% mAP@50, respectively, compared with 75.5% and 60.0% for YOLO26n. These results demonstrate a competitive accuracy--latency trade-off for edge deployment. Our code is available at https://github.com/XiaolongWU111/YOLOF---MLD/blob/main/README.md.

</details>


### [98] [Anatomical Prior-Guided Routing for 3D Lung Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39414)
*Uihyeon Maeng,Jonghun Kim,Hyunjin Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖先验引导路由模块（APRM），通过按patch的解剖先验确定性地选择路由门，在共享专家适配器池上做稀疏特征路由，实现区域特异的特征适配，提升3D肺肿瘤分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT中肺肿瘤体积占比小且在不同解剖位置呈现异质外观；基于patch的训练与滑窗推理使patch缺乏全局解剖上下文，放大了位置依赖的异质性，模型难以根据patch所属解剖区域自适应调整特征处理。

Method: 为每个图像patch提取区域占用的解剖先验信息，并据此确定性地选择一个解剖路由门；被选中的门在共享专家适配器池（expert adapter pool）上执行稀疏特征路由（可能为top-k/multi-gate路由），在跨区域共享专家容量的同时实现解剖特异的特征适配。在2个数据集、多种骨干网络上验证。

Result: 在两个数据集、多种骨干网络上均相对基线取得一致提升；特征可视化显示APRM的输出特征响应随patch位置变化，说明路由机制能依据解剖上下文自适应调整特征处理。

Conclusion: 解剖先验引导的确定性稀疏路由是缓解3D肺肿瘤分割中位置依赖异质性的有效手段，可与多种骨干网络即插即用地结合；未来可拓展至其他小目标/多区域器官分割任务。

Abstract: Accurate lung tumor segmentation in computed tomography (CT) is essential for treatment planning, response assessment, and quantitative imaging analysis. However, 3D lung tumor segmentation remains challenging because tumors occupy only a small portion of the whole volume and exhibit heterogeneous appearances across anatomical locations. In patch-based training and sliding-window inference for 3D medical image segmentation, location-dependent heterogeneity is further amplified because cropped image patches provide limited global anatomical context, making it difficult for a model to adapt its feature processing according to the anatomical region from which each patch is extracted. To address this limitation, we propose an Anatomical Prior-guided Routing Module (APRM) for lung tumor segmentation. For each image patch, we obtain anatomical prior information of regional occupancy and deterministically select an anatomical routing gate. The selected gate then performs sparse feature routing over a shared expert adapter pool, enabling anatomy-specific feature adaptation while sharing expert capacity across regions. We evaluate APRM on two datasets with various backbone networks and demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models. Furthermore, feature visualizations show that APRM produces different feature responses depending on patch location, indicating that the proposed routing mechanism effectively adapts feature processing according to anatomical context.

</details>


### [99] [Diagnosing the Reference Gap: What Visual Scaffolds Can and Cannot Fix in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39419)
*Guizhen Chen,Weiwen Xu,Wotao Yin,Anh Tuan Luu,Hou Pong Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出“指称缺口”（reference gap）概念，认为多模态大模型在视觉推理中的失败不完全源于感知，而是源于语言未能绑定到正确的视觉实体/区域/位置；并构建配对基准ReGap-Bench验证外部指称（如编号）可缩小该缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型能识别视觉内容，却难以进行多步可靠推理（如计数时“看到了却数丢”）。这类失败常被归因于感知能力不足，作者认为其本质是语言与视觉实体之间缺乏绑定，即指称缺口；并借鉴visual routines理论探究外部指称能否缓解该问题。

Method: 构建ReGap-Bench：包含五个视觉推理任务的配对基准，同一场景、问题与答案固定，仅改变指称方式（任务匹配指称 vs. 不匹配指称）。通过对照实验检验不同指称对模型准确率的影响。

Result: 任务匹配的指称最高可提升准确率约25%；不匹配指称收益很小，甚至导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 指称缺口是与视觉感知相区别的独立瓶颈；构造任务适配的视觉指称可作为一种免训练的视觉技能，用于提升多模态推理能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models can recognize visual content yet struggle to reason reliably over it across multiple steps. In counting, a model may see all objects but lose track of which ones it has counted. We argue that such failures, commonly attributed to perception, reveal a reference gap: the failure to bind language to the relevant visual entities, regions, or locations. Motivated by the theory of visual routines, we test whether external references such as numbering can narrow this gap. We introduce ReGap-Bench, a paired benchmark of five visual reasoning tasks in which the scene, question, and answer are fixed and only the reference varies. Results show that task-matched references improve accuracy by up to 25\%, while mismatched references provide little benefit or even hurt performance. These findings identify the reference gap as a bottleneck distinct from visual perception and show that constructing task-appropriate visual references can serve as a training-free visual skill for improving multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [FDSML: Formula-Driven Supervised Metric Learning with Parameter-Aware Triplet Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39423)
*Hina Otake,Shinei Arakawa,Keitaro Tanaka,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Shigeo Morishima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出公式驱动的监督度量学习（FDSML），利用图像生成参数导出关系型监督，配合FineFractalDB与参数感知三元组损失，提升检索迁移性并保持分类竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公式驱动监督学习（FDSL）将预训练建模为对离散标签的分类，丢弃了生成过程中的连续性信息，导致监督信号受限、向识别之外任务迁移不足。

Method: 构建FineFractalDB（类内变化更大、结构更稳定的精炼分形数据集）；提出参数感知三元组损失（PA-Triplet），依据迭代函数系统（IFS）参数空间中的距离分配正负样本，使嵌入服从参数导出的相对相似性序关系。

Result: ResNet-50上，Stanford Online Products的Recall@1由65.6%提升至74.0%，Stanford Cars由56.2%提升至78.4%；下游分类保持竞争力，多种损坏设置下鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 参数导出的关系型监督比基于标签的分类提供更强的合成预训练信号，尤其在识别之外的迁移任务上更有效。

Abstract: Formula-Driven Supervised Learning (FDSL) enables vision pre-training without real images, but existing methods cast pre-training as classification on discretized labels, discarding continuity in the generative process. We propose Formula-Driven Supervised Metric Learning (FDSML), which derives relational supervision from image-generation parameters beyond discrete class labels. FDSML combines FineFractalDB, our refined fractal dataset with higher intra-class variation and more stable structures, with a Parameter-Aware Triplet (PA-Triplet) Loss that assigns positives and negatives according to distances in the underlying Iterated Function System parameter space. This objective trains embeddings to respect parameter-derived relative similarity orderings. Experiments show that FDSML improves transferability to image retrieval while remaining competitive on downstream classification and improving robustness under several corruption settings. On ResNet-50, Recall@1 improves from 65.6% to 74.0% on Stanford Online Products and from 56.2% to 78.4% on Stanford Cars over FDSL pre-training. These results suggest that parameter-derived relational supervision provides a stronger synthetic pre-training signal than label-based classification for transfer beyond recognition.

</details>


### [101] [PathCowork: Learning from real-world pathology practice to build AI copilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39441)
*Sheng Wang,Jialu Yao,Maria V. Rossi,Shunsuke Koga,Esther Baranov,MacLean P. Nasrallah,Songhao Li,Jonathan Xu,Wei Du,Cindy McGrath,Timothy Chao,Anne Wang,Zhaohai Yang,Daniel Akuma,Selemon M. Walle,Olga Pozdnyakova,Edward B. Lee,Zahra Alipour,Annapurna Saksena,Rashmi Tondon,Nikolina Dioufa,David E. Elder,George J. Netto,Xiaowei Xu,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathCowork 通过同步采集病理学家的注视、口述与操作，将叙述性检查过程转化为视觉定位与诊断流程的监督数据，训练出服务端 PathCowork-1T 与本地 PathCowork-27B 智能体，在五项器官特异性评测中显著优于 Claude Code 与 Codex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 AI 主要学习医生记录的"答案"，而忽略了医生如何解读证据、推理得出结论的过程，导致监督信号贫乏、推理不透明。作者希望直接从常规临床实践中获取可扩展的过程性监督数据。

Method: 构建 PathCowork 框架，同步记录病理学家的眼动（gaze）、口述（narration）与图像导航（navigation），将视觉发现与其解读过程关联；采集 16 位病理学家 48 个会话、共 1028 分钟的全切片阅片数据，据此训练两个模型：面向服务器部署的 PathCowork-1T 与面向本地部署的 PathCowork-27B，强化视觉定位能力，并让智能体结合记录的诊断流程进行推理。

Result: 在五项器官特异性评测中，结合视觉定位与记录流程的智能体主任务评分分别比 Claude Code 和 Codex 高 17.9 与 24.1 个百分点，处理时间更短、推理成本更低；与最先进的多实例学习（MIL）AI 相比，在有限切片标签下 PathCowork 智能体提出的区域在前列腺分级上得分更高；此外还可用于构建病理 AI 基准，揭示模型在识别视野上下文与拒绝不适用前提方面的困难。

Conclusion: PathCowork 提出了一种通过诊断会话中的可解释推理来规模化采集数据并训练病理 AI 的新范式，为直接从常规临床实践中学习的系统开辟了路径。

Abstract: Medical AI learns from the answers doctors record, but rarely from how they interpret the evidence to reach them. Here we introduce PathCowork, a framework that turns pathologists' narrated examinations into supervision for visual grounding and procedures that guide diagnostic AI agents. Synchronized gaze, narration and navigation link visual findings to the process of interpreting them. Across 48 sessions from 16 pathologists, the platform captured 1,028 minutes of slide review. Using these data, we trained PathCowork-1T for server deployment and PathCowork-27B for local deployment, with improved visual grounding. Across five organ-specific evaluations, agents combining this grounding with recorded diagnostic procedures achieved primary task scores averaging 17.9 and 24.1 percentage points higher than Claude Code and Codex, respectively, with shorter processing times and lower inference costs. Compared to the state-of-the-art multiple-instance learning AIs, the PathCowork agent-proposed regions achieved higher prostate-grading scores with limited slide labels. PathCowork can also help construct pathology AI benchmarks where questions derived from the recordings identified difficulties in recognizing viewing context and rejecting inapplicable premises. In summary, PathCowork proposes a new paradigm to scale up the data collection and train pathology AI through transparent reasoning from diagnostic sessions, opening a path towards systems that learn directly from routine clinical practice.

</details>


### [102] [Calibrating Attention-Based Token Pruning for Efficient Multi-Image Synthesis in UMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39467)
*Zimeng Wu,Qijian Tian,Penghui Du,Changqian Yu,Jiaxin Chen,Yunhong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CATP 是首个面向统一多模态模型（UMM）生成任务的免训练上下文压缩方法，通过校准通用注意力 token 剪枝中的偏差，在多图合成场景下取得更优的质量-效率权衡，注意力核心加速最高 2.09 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMM 支持强大的多图合成，但长视觉上下文带来巨大计算开销。已有 token 剪枝方法多为理解任务适配而来，在生成任务中存在三个问题：参考图像探索不足、条件引导混乱、池化引入的歧义，导致生成质量下降。

Method: 基于去噪过程中逐层独立剪枝的框架，提出三项组件：(1) Guidance-Consistent Pruning (GCP)，在条件分支间同步剪枝决策以消除条件引导混乱；(2) Taylor-Calibrated Attention Metric (TCAM)，从理论上推导二阶修正项以抵消池化引起的注意力度量偏差；(3) Fast Scoring Operator (FSO)，将校准融合进分块在线 Softmax 以降低剪枝开销。整体为免训练方法。

Result: 在多种代表性 UMM 与基准上，CATP 一致优于现有 SOTA token 缩减方法，注意力核心最高实现 2.09× 加速。

Conclusion: 针对生成任务的特性（而非直接沿用理解任务的剪枝策略）进行校准，是提升 UMM 多图生成效率-质量权衡的有效途径；免训练上下文压缩可在不微调的前提下显著加速推理。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) enable powerful multi-image synthesis but incur substantial computation from long visual contexts. While token pruning offers a direct route to accelerating inference, existing methods are primarily adapted from understanding tasks, suffering from insufficient reference image exploration, confused conditional guidance, and pooling-induced ambiguity that degrades generation quality. To address these issues, we propose CATP, the first training-free context-compression method tailored to UMM generation, which \textbf{C}alibrates the discrepancies of general \textbf{A}ttention-based \textbf{T}oken \textbf{P}runing for better quality-efficiency trade-off. Concretely, building on independent layer-wise pruning during denoising, we introduce Guidance-Consistent Pruning (GCP) to synchronize pruning decisions across conditioning branches, and a Taylor-Calibrated Attention Metric (TCAM) that derives a theoretically grounded second-order correction for pooling-induced bias.
