In addition, a Fast Scoring Operator (FSO) fuses the calibration into tiled online Softmax to reduce pruning cost. Extensive experiments across representative UMMs and benchmarks demonstrate that CATP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art token-reduction methods, achieving up to 2.09$\times$ attention-core speedup.

</details>


### [103] [Regional Anchor Routing for Multi-Object Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39478)
*Yuze Li,Fuchen Long,Xinlong Sun,Cheng Yan,Xinyu Zhang,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RART 将逐物体运动提取、目标区域锚定与帧对齐注入统一到一个冻结的视频扩散 Transformer 中，实现无需逐视频优化的多物体组合式运动迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式运动迁移需从多个参考视频提取运动并分别施加到目标场景中用户指定的物体上。现有方法难以可靠地把不同参考运动分配给正确目标物体，导致串扰（意图作用于某物体的运动影响其他物体）；同时运动特定微调类方法在参考视频变化时必须重新优化，重复迁移效率低。

Method: 1) 对每个参考运动，从掩码参考视频中提取时序有序特征；2) 以目标首帧掩码为条件，构成"掩码锚定运动 token"，实现目标区域锚定；3) 在冻结视频扩散 Transformer 中引入帧对齐运动交叉注意力，使每个生成帧仅读取对应时间槽的运动 token，单次去噪过程中组合所有物体运动；4) 训练时引入运动注意力路由损失，促使各目标区域关注其对应运动 token，抑制物体间干扰。

Result: 实验表明 RART 实现了准确的运动保持、高效推理与较强的组合泛化能力；可处理未见过的参考—目标组合，且无需逐视频优化。

Conclusion: 通过统一物体级运动提取、掩码锚定与帧对齐注入，并配合注意力路由约束，RART 在保持基础模型冻结的前提下解决了多物体运动的归属歧义与重复优化开销问题，兼顾准确性与效率。

Abstract: Compositional motion transfer requires a model to extract motions from multiple reference videos, and apply each motion to a user-specified object in one target scene. Existing methods normally struggle to reliably assign different reference motions to the correct target objects, thus a motion intended for one object may affect another. Meanwhile, some motion-specific tuning approaches are still facing another issue: they must run a new optimization whenever the reference video changes, making repeated transfers inefficient. To address these limitations, we propose RART, a framework that unifies object-wise motion extraction, target-region anchoring, and frame-aligned injection into a frozen video Diffusion Transformer for multi-object motion transfer. For each reference motion, RART extracts temporally ordered features from the masked reference video, and then conditions them on the target first-frame mask to form mask-anchored motion tokens. Frame-aligned motion cross-attention allows each generated frame to read only the motion tokens at the corresponding time slots, composing all object motions in one denoising process. During training, a motion-attention routing loss encourages each target region to attend to its corresponding motion tokens and reduces interference between objects. Therefore, RART can handle novel reference-target combinations without per-video optimization. Experiments show that RART achieves accurate motion preservation, efficient inference, and strong compositional generalization.

</details>


### [104] [Agent-Wise Test-Time Normalization for Camera-Based Cooperative Perception with Corrupted Collaborators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39505)
*Yu Wang,Shaohua Wang,Yicheng Li,Kecheng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgentNorm：按智能体分别估计测试时归一化统计量，并将自车锚定到源域统计量，避免池化归一化在协同感知中把干净自车与受损协作者混合归一化所造成的自车性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间融合协同感知中，仅协作者相机受雾、运动模糊或曝光错误影响时，其共享特征偏离训练分布，而自车输入仍干净，导致协同反而劣于不协同。标准免训练补救方案——测试时批归一化（BN）自适应——在所有智能体的池化batch上估计统计量，用两分布混合去归一化干净自车与受损协作者；实验表明这种混合本身即损害自车：与受损协作者共享BN统计量时，自车基于自身特征的检测下降3.58–6.68 AP@0.5。

Method: AgentNorm为逐智能体测试时归一化：i) 每个协作者仅用自身当前观测估计归一化统计量；ii) 将自车锚定到其源域统计量，使自车通路严格保持原模型不变。无可学习参数、无需梯度或标签、与测试帧顺序无关、各智能体本地运行且不改变传输特征。

Result: 在基于相机的OPV2V（4个融合检查点、仅协作者受损）上，AgentNorm将severity-5雾下AP@0.5从29.42–37.25提升至43.32–58.86；雾与运动模糊下较池化归一化高5.53–12.94点；每帧额外延迟2.3 ms。在真实DAIR-V2X-C数据集上，于全部十种路侧单元受损设置中均优于池化归一化。

Conclusion: AgentNorm以极低开销有效修复"仅协作者受损"场景下的协同感知性能退化，并避免池化归一化对自车的污染。其失效边界明确：遮挡（移除图像内容而非偏移特征统计量）以及所有智能体共享同一偏移的情形不在修复范围内。

Abstract: Intermediate-fusion cooperative perception lets a vehicle detect objects beyond its own field of view by fusing features shared by nearby agents. When only a collaborator's cameras are degraded by fog, motion blur or exposure errors, the features it shares drift away from the training distribution while the ego input stays clean, and cooperation can become worse than not cooperating at all. Test-time batch-normalization adaptation, the standard training-free remedy, estimates statistics over the pooled batch of all agents and therefore normalizes both the clean ego and the corrupted collaborator with a mixture of two distributions. We show that this mixing harms even the clean ego: when the ego shares BN statistics with a corrupted collaborator, its detection from its own features drops by 3.58 to 6.68 AP@0.5 points although its cameras are untouched. We propose AgentNorm, an agent-wise test-time normalization that i) estimates the normalization statistics of every collaborator from its own current observation and ii) anchors the ego to its source statistics, so that the ego path remains exactly the original model. AgentNorm has no learnable parameters, needs no gradients or labels, is independent of the order of test frames, and runs locally on each agent without changing the transmitted features. On camera-based OPV2V with four fusion checkpoints and collaborator-only corruptions, AgentNorm raises AP@0.5 under severity-5 fog from 29.42-37.25 to 43.32-58.86 and outperforms pooled normalization by 5.53-12.94 points under fog and motion blur, at an extra latency of 2.3 ms per frame. On the real-world DAIR-V2X-C dataset, it outperforms pooled normalization in all ten infrastructure-corruption settings. We also report the cases it does not fix: occlusion, which removes image content rather than shifting feature statistics, and the case in which all agents share the same shift.

</details>


### [105] [Robust LIDAR Sensor-to-Sensor Domain Generalization via Accumulated-Point Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39516)
*Christoph B Rist,Larissa T Triess,Markus Enzweiler,Dariu M Gavrila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用源域训练、附加占用预测辅助目标的域泛化方法，用于LIDAR语义分割，在不改变输入、无目标域数据/标签、无推理开销下提升跨传感器泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实LIDAR存在传感器特定的采样模式，导致模型跨数据集迁移困难；而许多应用需在完全未见传感器上工作，且无法预先获取其数据或标签。

Method: 源域专用训练方案：将源域多帧扫描聚合为简单世界模型，作为占用预测（occupancy）辅助监督信号，替代单帧标准分割训练；推理时输入仍为单帧LIDAR，不修改输入、无额外运行时开销，辅助头可丢弃（纯分割）或保留（分割+场景补全）。

Result: 在多个不同LIDAR数据集上的实验表明，监督方式显著影响模型对新域的泛化；部分设置下无需任何显式域适应（无目标扫描、无目标标签、无传感器先验）且无预测细化即可提升未见域性能，同时保持有竞争力的源域分割性能，且无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: 监督信号的设计（而非仅输入或架构）是提升LIDAR语义分割跨传感器泛化的有效且低成本的途径。

Abstract: We study domain generalization for LIDAR semantic segmentation. Deep neural networks achieve strong performance on 3D point clouds, yet real-world LIDAR exhibits sensor-specific sampling patterns that hinder transfer across datasets. In many applications, performance on completely unseen sensors is crucial. A model should preferably work on a new setup without prior access to its data or labels. To this end, we introduce a source-only training scheme that adds an occupancy prediction auxiliary objective. We aggregate source-domain scans into a simple world model and use it as the supervision signal, replacing single-scan standard segmentation training.
  A key property of our approach is that it does not alter the input scan. During training and testing the network consumes the same single-frame LIDAR input, only the supervision signal is enriched via the occupancy objective. At deployment, there is no runtime overhead as the auxiliary head can be dropped for pure segmentation, or kept to obtain scene completion alongside semantic segmentation. In an experimental evaluation across distinct LIDAR datasets, we demonstrate that a model's generalization to new domains can be significantly affected by how it is supervised. In certain setups, performance on unseen domains improves without any explicit domain adaptation (no target scans, no target labels, no sensor priors) and without prediction refinement. At the same time, our method maintains competitive source-domain segmentation performance and incurs no additional inference cost.

</details>


### [106] [FFAI: Flow Matching with Feedback Training and Adaptive Initialization for CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39517)
*Jigang Duan,Heran Wang,Genwei Ma,Xing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一条件流匹配方法，通过反馈感知训练与退化自适应初始化，统一解决低剂量、稀疏视角与有限角度CT重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量、稀疏视角、有限角度CT退化机制不同，但均因测量信息受限而难以重建，可纳入统一框架；现有基于流匹配的迭代图像复原中，预测误差会使推理状态偏离训练轨迹，且固定初始化难以适应退化程度不同的输入。

Method: 构建以初始重建为条件的统一条件流匹配框架。训练阶段引入教师网络模拟推理更新以生成偏移状态，学生网络从中学习恢复高质量参考图像，提升对预测误差的鲁棒性（反馈感知训练）；推理阶段依据噪声水平或投影数据缺失程度选择起始时间，并相应调整初始状态所加噪声，实现退化自适应初始化。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上，该方法在三类重建任务中的定量指标均优于对比方法，能有效抑制噪声与伪影，同时保留解剖结构与细节。

Conclusion: 反馈感知训练与退化自适应初始化相结合的统一条件流匹配框架，可有效应对推理偏差与退化差异，为多类受限测量CT重建提供统一解决方案。

Abstract: Although low-dose, sparse-view, and limited-angle CT involve distinct degradation mechanisms, all three pose reconstruction challenges due to limited measurement information and can therefore be addressed within a unified framework. In iterative image restoration with flow matching, prediction errors may cause inference states to deviate from the prescribed training path, while a fixed initialization may be unsuitable for inputs with varying degradation severity. We propose a unified conditional flow matching method that conditions on the initial reconstruction and combines feedback-aware training with degradation-adaptive initialization. During training, a teacher network simulates inference updates to generate shifted states, from which a student network learns to recover high-quality reference images, improving its ability to handle prediction errors. During inference, the starting time is selected based on the noise level or extent of missing projection data, and the noise added to the initial state is adjusted accordingly to adapt the starting point to the input. Experiments on public benchmark datasets show that the proposed method achieves better quantitative results than the compared methods across all three reconstruction tasks, effectively suppressing noise and artifacts while preserving anatomical structures and details.

</details>


### [107] [BRIDGE: Learning Brain-Gut Metabolic Evidence for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39520)
*Pengli Zhu,Ruxi Chang,Tianliang Yao,Yu Jiang,Shengyuan Liu,Yiyang Lin,Xiaoyi Duan,Shuang Wu,Yixuan Yuan,the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BRIDGE两阶段框架，将脑-肠代谢知识迁移到仅用脑PET的阿尔茨海默病诊断中，在CN/MCI与CN/AD分类上较纯脑基线AUC分别提升6.0%与5.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD诊断可受益于脑-肠相互作用相关的肠道代谢信息，但现有模型多仅分析脑葡萄糖代谢；全身FDG-PET虽可提供成对的脑-肠观测，但常规脑检查几乎不含肠道成像，导致该信息难以直接用于诊断。

Method: BRIDGE为两阶段框架：①条件式脑→肠潜空间传输模块（conditional brain-to-gut latent transport）与疾病证据引导的脑通路，学习受试者特异的跨器官对应关系与疾病相关脑表征；②脑通路中借助LLM将文献描述整理为经人工审核的语义锚点以引导特征学习；③反事实引导的残差融合机制，用匹配/非匹配虚拟肠道证据监督置信门控，动态调节传输信息的贡献。推理阶段仅需脑PET。

Result: 在公开与私有队列上的大量实验表明BRIDGE优于纯脑与跨器官基线；相对纯脑对应方法，CN/MCI与CN/AD诊断AUC分别提升6.0%和5.2%。

Conclusion: BRIDGE可在推理时仅以脑PET为输入，迁移互补的脑-肠代谢上下文，兼顾性能与临床可部署性，具备实际诊断潜力。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis may benefit from gut metabolic information associated with brain-gut interactions, yet existing models largely analyze cerebral glucose metabolism alone. Although whole-body fluorodeoxyglucose positron emission tomography (FDG-PET) can provide paired brain-gut observations, routine brain examinations rarely include gut imaging, limiting direct use of this information for diagnosis. In this paper, we present BRIDGE (Brain and Gut Representation Integration via Distribution-Guided Enhancement), a two-stage framework for transferring brain-gut metabolic knowledge to brain-only diagnosis. Specifically, BRIDGE integrates a conditional brain-to-gut latent transport module and a disease-evidence-guided cerebral pathway to capture subject-specific cross-organ correspondence and disease-relevant cerebral representations. For the cerebral pathway, a large language model (LLM) helps organize literature-derived descriptors into human-approved semantic anchors that guide feature learning. Additionally, we introduce a counterfactual-guided residual fusion mechanism, which uses matched and non-matched virtual-gut evidence to supervise a confidence gate and dynamically regulate the contribution of transported cross-organ information. At inference, BRIDGE exploits complementary gut-related context using brain PET alone, without measured gut PET. Extensive experiments across public and private cohorts demonstrate that BRIDGE outperforms brain-only and cross-organ baselines, achieving AUC gains of 6.0% and 5.2% over its brain-only counterpart for CN/MCI and CN/AD diagnosis, respectively. Notably, its ability to transfer complementary brain-gut metabolic context while preserving brain PET as the sole inference input highlights its potential for clinically practical diagnosis. Our code is available at https://github.com/CUHK-AIM-Group/BRIDGE.

</details>


### [108] [MorphSIG: Subject-Driven Image Generation via Decoupled Anchoring and Feature Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39530)
*Hailong Yan,Yongrui Zhang,Xiangtao Zhang,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorphSIG：免训练框架，将主体驱动图像生成重构为伪视频特征传输，通过频域解耦锚定与伪光流引导的潜空间特征传输，提升姿态多样性与主体保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动图像生成（SIG）方法存在结构锁定导致姿态多样性不足、风格同质化，且基准缺乏真实标注（ground truth）。

Method: 两个核心模块：(1) 渐进解耦锚定：在早期去噪阶段采用频域解耦与结构加扰，打破结构锁定，实现从自由构图到身份对齐的渐进过渡；(2) 伪光流引导特征传输：构建潜空间伪流场，模拟图生视频的时序一致性，精确传输主体语义。另构建含多样化风格化主体的基准。

Result: 大量实验表明，无需额外训练即可显著提升姿态多样性与主体保真度，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MorphSIG 弥合静态生成与动态传播之间的鸿沟，为高保真主体一致性生成提供了一种高性价比范式。

Abstract: We propose MorphSIG, a training-free framework that reformulates subject-driven image generation (SIG) as pseudo-video feature transport. MorphSIG contains two core modules: Progressive Decoupled Anchoring, which uses frequency-domain decoupling and structural scrambling during early denoising to break structural locking and enable a gradual transition from free composition to identity alignment; and Pseudo-flow Guided Feature Transport, which constructs a latent-space pseudo-flow field to mimic Image-to-Video temporal coherence and transport subject semantics precisely. To address stylistic homogeneity and the lack of ground truth in existing benchmarks, we further introduce a benchmark with diverse stylized subjects. Extensive experiments show that MorphSIG significantly improves pose diversity and subject fidelity over baselines without additional training. Overall, MorphSIG bridges static generation and dynamic propagation, providing a cost-effective paradigm for high-fidelity subject consistency.

</details>


### [109] [MOSAIC-Flow: Orthogonal-Axis and Status-Conditioned Fusion for Missing-Sequence Completion in Brain Tumor MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39534)
*Pengli Zhu,Zhihao Peng,Yixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC-Flow统一框架用于脑肿瘤MRI缺失序列补全：以模态感知编码与正交轴细化保留三维细节，用状态条件原型融合实现目标自适应条件化，并以条件流匹配生成缺失序列，在UCSF-PDGM八项单目标补全任务上取得稳定全脑与肿瘤区域保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤全面表征依赖多序列MRI，但跨模态融合易模糊序列特异对比度并丢失细粒度三维肿瘤细节，需要一种既保留序列特异信息又能高质量补全缺失序列的方法。

Method: MOSAIC-Flow：模态感知stem独立编码各序列；正交轴细化在保持三维表征的同时建模轴位、冠状位、矢状位依赖；状态条件原型融合用紧凑的模态token与可用性token，对融合视觉表征的池化查询进行路由与调制，实现目标自适应条件化而避免稠密体素级注意力；以条件流匹配目标将目标通道噪声输运至单一缺失序列，七个已获取体数据在输运过程中保持固定，并对四条Heun轨迹取平均得到补全结果。

Result: 在UCSF-PDGM的结构、扩散与增强MRI上评估，八项单目标补全任务均表现出一致的全脑与肿瘤区域保真度，结构序列上性能最强。

Conclusion: MOSAIC-Flow以目标条件化流匹配替代跨模态稠密融合，可在保留序列特异对比与三维细节的前提下统一完成缺失序列补全，且在结构序列上效果最佳。

Abstract: Missing-sequence completion is important for comprehensive brain tumor characterization, but cross-modal fusion can obscure sequence-specific contrast and lose fine-grained three-dimensional tumor detail. To address this challenge, we introduce Modality-aware Orthogonal-axis Status-conditioned Attentive Integration for Completion (MOSAIC-Flow), a unified framework for missing-sequence completion that learns a target-conditioned velocity field while keeping the seven acquired volumes fixed throughout the transport. Specifically, modality-aware stems independently encode each sequence, and orthogonal-axis refinement captures axial, coronal, and sagittal dependencies while retaining the three-dimensional representation. Status-conditioned prototype fusion uses compact modality and availability tokens to route and modulate pooled queries from the fused visual representation, enabling target-adaptive conditioning without dense voxel-to-voxel attention. A conditional flow matching objective transports target-channel noise toward the single missing sequence, while four Heun trajectories are averaged to produce the completion. Evaluation across structural, diffusion, and contrast-enhanced MRI in UCSF-PDGM demonstrates consistent whole-brain and tumor-region fidelity across all eight single-target completion tasks, with the strongest performance on structural sequences. Code is available at https://github.com/CUHK-AIM-Group/MOSAIC-FLOW.

</details>


### [110] [ROAD: Modern Adversarial Training for Simple One-Step Distillation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39539)
*Xiaoou Li,Shan You,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROAD：单阶段纯对抗蒸馏框架，用轻量表征空间判别器一步蒸馏文生图扩散模型，在SDXL上COCO-10K FID 11.03，SD3.5-M上13.73，超越多步教师且训练算力远低于分布匹配法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步蒸馏可把预训练扩散模型变成快速生成器，但强性能往往依赖大量训练机制与算力；纯对抗蒸馏虽更简洁，却难以扩展到现代文生图规模。论文旨在降低训练复杂度与算力，同时保持高质量一步生成。

Method: ROAD为单阶段框架，仅用一个对抗目标与轻量表征空间critic：（1）以Lipschitz受控的Transformer判别头替代卷积头，并引入token级文本条件，以匹配现代视觉编码器的语义token；（2）发现固定随机特征混合会导致长程退化，归因于生成器与判别器围绕单一实现视图的协同适应，故周期性重采样混合；（3）针对稠密向量值logits，提出Jacobian R1，惩罚完整输入-logit雅可比，克服pooled R1忽略多数logit敏感性的缺陷。

Result: 在COCO-10K上，ROAD单步生成在SDXL上FID为11.03，在SD3.5-M上为13.73，超过其多步教师模型，且训练算力仅为分布匹配方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: 单一对抗目标配合表征空间判别器与上述三项设计，足以实现现代化的高质量一步文生图蒸馏，兼顾效果、简洁性与训练效率。

Abstract: One-step distillation turns pretrained diffusion models into fast generators, but strong results often require substantial training machinery and compute. Pure adversarial distillation offers a simpler route, yet has struggled at modern text-to-image scale. To address this limitation, we propose ROAD (Representation-grounded One-step Adversarial Distillation), a one-stage framework that distills text-to-image models with one adversarial objective and a lightweight representation-space critic. First, we replace the classical convolutional head with a Lipschitz-controlled Transformer head with token-level text conditioning, which matches the semantic tokens of modern visual encoders. Second, we observed a fixed random feature mixing leads to long-horizon degradation. We attribute it to generator-critic co-adaptation around one realization-specific view, and periodically re-draw the mixing to keep the randomization effective. Third, for our dense and vector-valued logits, we introduce Jacobian R1 which penalizes the full input-logit Jacobian, overcoming pooled R1's limitation of neglecting most of logit sensitivities. A single adversarial objective then suffices: in one step, ROAD reaches 11.03 FID on SDXL and 13.73 on SD3.5-M on COCO-10K, surpassing the multi-step teachers at a fraction of distribution matching's training compute.

</details>


### [111] [Comprehensive empirical evaluation of feature extractors in computer vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39551)
*Murat Isik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对SIFT、SURF、BRIEF、ORB、BRISK、KAZE、AKAZE、FREAK、DAISY、FAST、STAR等传统特征检测与描述子进行系统性评测；在IMC Photo Tourism 2020（150万+图像）上，FAST+ORB+BF最快，ORB擅仿射与增亮，AKAZE擅模糊、鱼眼、旋转与透视畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征检测与匹配是计算机视觉的基础环节，支撑大量应用；但传统方法众多、架构与复杂度各异，缺乏在统一大规模数据与统一变换条件（旋转、尺度、模糊、亮度、仿射、透视、鱼眼、椒盐噪声）下的横向可比性评估，因此需要一次全面的基准比较以指导方法选择。

Method: 选取11种传统检测器/描述子（SIFT、SURF、BRIEF、ORB、BRISK、KAZE、AKAZE、FREAK、DAISY、FAST、STAR），从架构设计与计算复杂度两方面分析其对效率和鲁棒性的影响；在Image Matching Challenge Photo Tourism 2020数据集（超过150万图像）上进行累计超过200万次对比实验，考察旋转、缩放、模糊、增亮、仿射变换、透视畸变、鱼眼畸变与椒盐噪声等条件下的表现，并比较检测器与描述子、匹配器（如Brute-Force）组合的效果。

Result: FAST检测器配合ORB描述子与BF匹配器在特征提取与匹配速度上最快，为效率最优组合；ORB在仿射变换与增亮图像上表现突出；AKAZE在模糊、鱼眼畸变、图像旋转与透视畸变条件下更优越；通过大规模对比明确了各提取器在不同退化条件下的鲁棒性差异。

Conclusion: 传统特征方法并不存在全局最优解，应依据计算预算与目标退化类型进行选择：追求实时效率可选FAST+ORB+BF，仿射/光照变化优先ORB，复杂几何与模糊/鱼眼/旋转/透视场景优先AKAZE；该研究为应用中的检测器与描述子选型提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Feature detection and matching are fundamental components in computer vision, underpinning a broad spectrum of applications. This study offers a comprehensive evaluation of traditional feature detections and descriptors, analyzing methods such as Scale Invariant Feature Transform (SIFT), Speeded-Up Robust Features (SURF), Binary Robust Independent Elementary Features (BRIEF), Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB), Binary Robust Invariant Scalable Keypoints (BRISK), KAZE, Accelerated KAZE (AKAZE), Fast Retina Keypoint (FREAK), Dense and Accurate Invariant Scalable descriptor for Yale (DAISY), Features from Accelerated Segment Test (FAST), and STAR. Each feature extractor was assessed based on its architectural design and complexity, focusing on how these factors influence computational efficiency and robustness under various transformations. Utilizing the Image Matching Challenge Photo Tourism 2020 dataset, which includes over 1.5 million images, the study identifies the FAST algorithm as the most efficient detector when paired with the ORB descriptor and Brute-Force (BF) matcher, offering the fastest feature extraction and matching process. ORB is notably effective on affine transformed and brightened images, while AKAZE excels in conditions involving blurring, fisheye distortion, image rotation, and perspective distortions. Through more than 2 million comparisons, the study highlights the feature extractors that demonstrate superior resilience across various conditions, including rotation, scaling, blurring, brightening, affine transformations, perspective distortions, fisheye distortion, and salt-and-pepper noise

</details>


### [112] [BEACON: Distilled Begin/End-Aware Structured Decoding for Continuous Sign Language Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39553)
*Guohou Zhang,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BEACON分割器（BIOE四类标注+8种合法转移的约束Viterbi解码+二分教师蒸馏的边界知识），在严格无cut评估下大幅提升segment-F1/boundary-F1并降低下游WER，并论证segment-F1比boundary-F1更能反映识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续手语识别中分割只是服务于下游识别的手段，但现有分割器通常只用局部边界检测指标评价，而非用词汇段质量及其支撑的识别效果来评价，导致评价目标与真实用途脱节。

Method: BEACON：1) 四类Outside/Begin/Inside/End (BIOE) 标注；2) 在8种合法转移上做约束Viterbi解码；3) 将二分类教师模型的边界知识蒸馏到共享学生模型。采用严格无cut评估与基于验证集的模型选择；并横跨五个架构家族比较分割指标与下游WER的相关性（Spearman ρ）。

Result: 相比SOTA，segment-F1提升14.58/14.59点，boundary-F1提升2.17/1.22点，下游WER降低9.10/4.15点（PHOENIX-14/BSL Corpus）。segment-F1与WER的一致性优于boundary-F1（Spearman ρ = -1.0/-0.9 对 -0.9/-0.8）。

Conclusion: 手语分割应以段质量和下游识别效用为评价标准，而非仅看局部边界准确率；segment-F1是更可靠的分割评价指标。

Abstract: In segmentation-based continuous sign language recognition, an untrimmed video is first partitioned into lexical units; segmentation is therefore a means to an end---downstream recognition. However, segmenters are usually judged by local boundary detection rather than by the quality of the lexical segments and the recognition they enable. We present BEACON, a begin/end-aware segmenter that combines four-class Outside/Begin/Inside/End (BIOE) labeling, constrained Viterbi decoding over eight legal transitions, and boundary knowledge distilled from a binary teacher into a shared student. Under strict no-cut evaluation with validation-based model selection, BEACON improves segment-F1 by 14.58/14.59 points and boundary-F1 by 2.17/1.22 points, and reduces the downstream word error rate (WER) by 9.10/4.15 points relative to the state-of-the-art in the PHOENIX-14/BSL Corpus. To examine which segmentation metric better reflects downstream recognition, we evaluated five distinct architecture families. Segment-F1 aligns more consistently with lower WER than boundary-F1 in both corpora (Spearman $ρ=-1.0/-0.9$ versus $-0.9/-0.8$). These results support evaluating sign language segmentation in terms of segment quality and downstream recognition utility rather than local boundary accuracy alone.

</details>


### [113] [DOSE: Dominance-Guided Ordinal Severity Estimation from Sparse Relative Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39584)
*Tushir Sahu,Hideaki Hayashi,Hajime Nagahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DOSE 提出仅用稀疏成对比较标注学习序数医学严重度估计的框架：通过全局比较图估计支配分数、以分数差设定嵌入空间累积间隔并生成伪对监督标量严重度头，再用无标签分数校准（tie-anchored segmentation）转成离散等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学严重度等级本质上是序数的，但现有方法常把等级当作独立类别处理。Learning-to-rank 可用基于图像对严重度比较的相对标注训练，能给出连续严重度排序，却无法直接预测离散严重度等级；因此需要一种既能利用稀疏相对标注、又能输出离散等级的方法。

Method: 1) 通过全局比较图聚合观察到的成对比较，为每张图像估计支配分数（dominance score）；2) 用支配分数差在嵌入空间中设定累积间隔，使估计严重度差异更大的对获得更强的分离；3) 由分数差生成伪对，与观察到的比较一起监督一个标量严重度头；4) 通过无标签分数校准将学到的连续严重度轴转成离散等级，主校准方法为 tie-anchored segmentation。

Result: 在 ACNE04、IDRiD、LIMUC 三个数据集上，不同 pair 预算下 DOSE 均稳定优于成对排序基线。

Conclusion: DOSE 表明可以在完全不使用绝对等级标注的条件下，从稀疏成对比较中学习序数医学严重度，并借助无标签校准直接获得离散严重度等级，性能优于成对排序基线；代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical severity estimation commonly relies on absolute grade annotations, which are fundamentally ordinal, yet existing methods often treat the grades as independent categories. Learning-to-rank methods, which can be trained using relative annotations based on severity comparisons of image pairs, can provide continuous severity rankings but cannot directly predict discrete severity grades. We propose Dominance-guided Ordinal Severity Estimation from Sparse Relative Annotations (DOSE), a framework for learning ordinal medical severity from sparse pairwise comparisons without using absolute annotations. DOSE aggregates the observed comparisons through a global comparison graph to estimate a dominance score for each image. Differences between dominance scores set cumulative margins in the embedding space, so that pairs with larger estimated severity differences are separated more strongly, and also generate pseudo-pairs that supervise a scalar severity head alongside the observed comparisons. The learned continuous severity axis is converted into discrete grades via label-free score calibration, with tie-anchored segmentation as the main calibration method. In the experiment, DOSE consistently outperformed pairwise-ranking baselines on ACNE04, IDRiD, and LIMUC under different pair budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/TushirSahu/DOSE

</details>


### [114] [TactoHand: Learning Geometry-Based Dense Hand-Surface Interaction Fields from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39585)
*Ziheng Xi,Weibo Li,Jiayi Yu,Sipeng Zheng,Wanpeng Zhang,Haiweng Xu,Hao Luo,Chuan Mao,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TactoHand 从单目视频预测手-表面三种稠密交互场（接触、接近度、3D 力），无需触觉硬件即可大规模学习手-物交互表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类与世界的交互需要在手部表面表征物理交互。触觉传感可捕捉此类交互，但大规模采集触觉数据依赖专用硬件，成本高、难扩展。

Method: 构建约 5000 万源帧的精选数据集；以几何方式推导接触与接近度标签，以运动信息推导力标签。模型采用时序 Transformer 聚合跨帧视觉证据，用三个可学习 query 解码对应的手-表面场，并引入可微分物理层，利用观测到的物体运动约束力预测。

Result: 在域内与域外基准上，接触与接近度估计优于已有方法；物理监督降低运动 rollout 的位置与旋转误差；预测的接触与接近度场提升下游手-物重建效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明可在不采集触觉测量的前提下，以可扩展、低成本的方式学习手-表面交互场。

Abstract: Understanding how humans interact with the world requires a representation of physical interactions at the hand surface. Tactile sensing captures these interactions, but collecting tactile data at scale requires specialized hardware. We introduce TactoHand, a model that predicts three dense hand-surface interaction fields---contact, proximity, and 3D force---from monocular video. We derive geometry-based contact and proximity labels and motion-derived force labels from eligible training data within a curated collection spanning approximately 50 million source frames. TactoHand uses a temporal transformer to aggregate visual evidence across frames, while three learned queries decode the corresponding hand-surface fields. A differentiable physics layer further constrains force predictions using observed object motion. Across in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, TactoHand improves contact and proximity estimation over prior methods. In addition, physics supervision reduces position and rotation errors in motion rollouts, and the predicted contact and proximity fields improve downstream hand-object reconstruction. Together, these results show a scalable, low-cost path to learning hand-surface interaction fields without collecting tactile measurements. Code will be released at https://github.com/zhenqis123/Tactohand.

</details>


### [115] [Operator-Aware Posterior Sampling for Multivariate Weather Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39592)
*Carlo Saccardi,Rudolf Saathof,Geethu Joseph,Justin Dauwels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将降尺度建模为贝叶斯逆问题，在学习到的前向算子下结合逐通道梯度归一化的引导，实现单一强度跨变量有效的扩散先验降尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散先验的贝叶斯逆问题框架可解耦先验与粗源，使单一预训练模型适配不同数据源；但存在两难点：(1) 粗细场来自不同再分析系统时，无固定粗化算子可刻画其关系；(2) 各变量引导梯度量级相差数个数量级，单一引导强度失效。

Method: 不改动先验，仅在引导循环内改进：利用配对数据学习前向算子（替代固定粗化算子）；对引导梯度做逐通道归一化，并论证该归一化等价于块最大范数（block-max norm）下的最速下降，从而可用统一引导强度。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果；方法层面表明学习到的算子能刻画跨再分析系统的关系，逐通道归一化使单一引导强度在各变量上均可有效工作。

Conclusion: 通过在引导环节引入可学习前向算子与逐通道梯度归一化，提升了扩散先验降尺度的跨源灵活性与变量间稳健性，先验模型保持复用不变；代码已开源。

Abstract: Statistical downscaling is the task of reconstructing high-resolution meteorological fields from coarse inputs. A principled approach formulates downscaling as a Bayesian inverse problem, using a diffusion prior over high-resolution fields and incorporating the coarse field only at inference through a likelihood. This decouples the prior from the coarse source, allowing one pretrained model to adapt to different sources. However, two challenges limit this flexibility: (1) when coarse and target fields come from different reanalysis systems, no fixed coarsening operator can capture their relationship; and (2) guidance gradients can differ by orders of magnitude across variables, making a single guidance strength ineffective. This paper addresses both challenges within the guidance loop, leaving the prior unchanged. We learn the forward operator from paired data and normalize the gradient per channel. We show that this normalization corresponds to steepest descent under a block-max norm, allowing a single guidance strength across variables. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/CarloSaccardi/operator-aware-weather-downscaling.

</details>


### [116] [Evaluating Cultural Knowledge Editing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39602)
*Malvina Nikandrou,Georgios Pantazopoulos,Ioannis Konstas,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CulturalBind 基准（3.2K 图像、1K 概念、6 个领域），用于评估 VLM 的文化感知跨模态知识与概念级知识编辑，发现文本与视觉知识呈解耦状态，现有编辑方法跨模态迁移性差且序列编辑下性能快速退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型（VLM）难以识别文化特定概念，尤其来自欠代表性区域的长尾知识；如何有效将这类长尾知识注入现有模型仍是难题，作者因此考察"知识编辑"作为针对性替代方案。

Method: 构建 CulturalBind 基准，覆盖 6 个领域的 1K 概念、3.2K 图像，用于评测 VLM 的文化感知跨模态知识与概念级知识编辑；先进行编辑前评测以考察文本知识与视觉知识的耦合情况，再在单次与序列两种编辑场景下评测现有知识编辑方法。

Result: 编辑前评测显示文本与视觉知识解耦：模型常具备文化概念的文本知识，却无法从图像中识别；现有编辑方法虽能纠正单个视觉识别错误，但跨模态可迁移性低，无法将编辑整合进模型更广泛的参数化知识；序列编辑下性能迅速下降。

Conclusion: 现有知识编辑方法不足以支撑文化长尾知识的多模态注入，亟需能随时间更好地巩固多模态知识的综合适应方法。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently fail to recognize culture-specific concepts, especially from underrepresented regions. Effectively injecting this long-tail knowledge into existing models remains a challenge. In this work, we investigate knowledge editing as a targeted alternative by introducing CulturalBind, a benchmark comprising 3.2K images of 1K concepts from 6 domains, designed to evaluate culture-aware cross-modal knowledge as well as concept-level knowledge editing in VLMs. Pre-edit evaluation reveals that textual and visual knowledge is decoupled: models often demonstrate text-based knowledge of cultural concepts but fail to identify them from images. We then evaluate editing methods in single and sequential scenarios. Although current methods successfully correct individual visual recognition errors, they exhibit low cross-modal portability, failing to integrate edits into the model's broader parametric knowledge. Furthermore, performance degrades rapidly under sequential editing, highlighting the need for comprehensive adaptation approaches that better consolidate multimodal knowledge over time.

</details>


### [117] [DiFuse: Fusing Native Diffusion Representations for Spatial Reasoning in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39609)
*Ruixin Fan,Weining Wang,Jiahui Sun,Tailai Chen,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiFuse，将UMM原生扩散分支的中间表示经空间对齐后选择性注入多模态理解通路，以增强空间推理且不损害通用理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型中理解与生成之间的信息流高度不对称：理解表示常用于条件化生成，而生成表示很少被复用于多模态推理，浪费了扩散分支可能蕴含的空间信息。

Method: 通过逐层分析定位最具信息量的扩散表示及其低干扰注入位置；将扩散特征与视觉token做空间对齐，并以残差融合方式注入选定的多模态解码器层。

Result: 在UniWorld-V1上相对匹配的SFT基线稳定提升空间推理，通用多模态基准基本持平；消融显示增益关键依赖扩散表示选择、注入位置及扩散特征与视觉token的空间对应关系；在OmniGen2上验证了跨架构可迁移性。

Conclusion: 以结构保持方式选择性整合原生扩散表示，可为多模态推理提供互补的空间线索，是缓解理解—生成信息流不对称的可行路径。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) jointly support visual understanding and image generation, yet information flow between these two capabilities remains largely asymmetric: understanding representations routinely condition generation, whereas generative representations are rarely reused for multimodal reasoning. In this work, we investigate whether intermediate representations from a UMM's native diffusion branch can enhance spatial reasoning without compromising general multimodal understanding. We propose DiFuse, which selectively incorporates intermediate diffusion representations into the understanding pathway of UMMs. Through systematic layer-wise analysis, we identify which diffusion representations are most informative and where they can be integrated with minimal interference to semantic processing. Guided by these findings, DiFuse spatially aligns diffusion features with visual tokens and injects them into selected multimodal decoder layers via residual fusion. Extensive experiments on UniWorld-V1 show that, DiFuse consistently improves spatial reasoning over a matched SFT baseline while maintaining broadly comparable performance on general multimodal benchmarks. Further analyses show that these gains depend critically on the choice of diffusion representations, injection positions, and the preservation of spatial correspondence between diffusion features and visual tokens. Results on OmniGen2 further demonstrate the transferability of DiFuse across unified multimodal architectures. Overall, our findings show that native diffusion representations can provide complementary spatial cues for multimodal reasoning when selectively integrated in a structure-preserving manner.

</details>


### [118] [UniTele: Learning Device-Conditioned Degradations for Unified Smartphone Telephoto Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39617)
*Yuchen Zhang,Aojie Li,Wei Cheng,Xianfang Zeng,Runxing Liu,haixin hao,Gang YU,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可分解的设备退化建模方法，用少量真实拍摄对学习设备相关退化并合成训练数据，训练统一的超长焦超分模型 UniTele。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机超长焦成像存在复杂且强设备相关的退化，手工设计的退化管线难以刻画；而跨机型、跨场景采集大规模对齐训练对成本高、难扩展。

Method: 将退化学习分解为两个可学习分量：一个刻画良好分辨条件下的设备成像特性，另一个刻画超长焦成像额外的信息损失与伪影。构建覆盖华为、苹果、vivo 三品牌的配对基准：同一内容在两种显示尺寸与变焦设置下拍摄，大图以手机最大光学变焦拍摄，小图以最高原生变焦经原生成像管线直拍，含 519 训练对与 60 测试对。用该基准学习设备相关退化，再迁移到外部图像合成数千配对，训练统一 SR 模型 UniTele。

Result: 在留出基准上，相对未适配的 Qwen 与 FLUX 预训练骨干，完整 UniTele 训练配方显著提升配对复原保真度与感知相似度，复原结果更稳定、视觉更忠实。

Conclusion: 少量真实拍摄即可有效学习设备相关退化并迁移合成训练数据，为统一超长焦超分提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Super-telephoto imaging on mobile phones suffers from complex, device-dependent degradations that are poorly captured by conventional handcrafted pipelines. Collecting large-scale aligned training pairs across different phones and diverse scenes, however, is both costly and difficult to scale. We therefore study whether device-specific degradations can be learned from only a small number of physical captures and transferred for training a unified super-resolution (SR) model. Our key idea is to factor degradation learning into two learnable components: one captures the device-specific imaging characteristics under well-resolved conditions, while the other captures the additional information loss and artifacts that arise in super-telephoto imaging. We build a paired benchmark across three phone brands (Huawei, Apple, and vivo) by photographing the same content at two display sizes and zoom settings. The larger displayed image is captured at the phone's maximum optical zoom setting, while the smaller image is photographed directly at the phone's highest native zoom setting through the phone's native imaging pipeline. The benchmark contains 519 training and 60 held-out pairs. We use the benchmark to learn device-specific degradation, then apply it to external images to synthesize thousands of additional pairs to train our UniTele SR model. Relative to the pretrained Qwen and FLUX backbones before adaptation, the full UniTele training recipe substantially improves paired restoration fidelity and perceptual similarity on the held-out benchmark and yields markedly more stable and visually faithful restorations.

</details>


### [119] [FutureWorld: A Plug-and-Play Causal Next-State Predictor for Interactive Video World Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39619)
*Guangyuan Li,Kelu Yao,Shuolin Xu,Xinlei Yu,Wei Xing,Lei Zhao,Peng-Tao Jiang,Xiaogang Xu,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的因果下一块预测器 C-NCP，在生成下一交互视频块前预测未来场景状态作为先验引导，缓解基于历史潜变量的交互式视频世界模型中的误差回流与累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于历史的交互视频生成方法复用已生成潜变量以保持场景一致性，但存在三点缺陷导致误差回流：1）每步生成的潜变量无筛选地被保留为历史，可能含模糊细节、几何畸变或语义漂移；2）检索到的历史内容虽与当前场景匹配，仍可能将错误带入下一块；3）新输出又成为后续历史，通过反复复用强化同类错误。作者希望打破这一循环。

Method: 在生成下一交互视频块之前，依据当前生成潜变量与下一动作预测未来场景状态，形成"未来状态先验"以引导下一块生成；据此设计即插即用的 Causal Next-Chunk Predictor（C-NCP），具备跨模型适配能力与预测引导调制机制，可嵌入多种基础生成器。

Result: 将 C-NCP 集成到四种基础生成器，并在交互式长视频生成与动作条件世界建模的三个基准上评测；大量实验表明在不同生成设置下误差累积降低、长时程一致性提升。

Conclusion: 以"预测未来状态"替代单纯"复用历史"作为引导信号，可有效抑制历史误差的循环放大，且方法通用、即插即用，适用于多种交互式视频世界模型基座。

Abstract: Interactive video world models aim to follow user actions while maintaining scene consistency over extended interactions. History-based methods reuse generated latents to preserve scene content, but three limitations of this reuse contribute to error recirculation. 1) The latent generated at each interaction is retained as history, potentially containing blurred details, geometric distortions, or semantic drift. 2) Although retrieved historical content matches the current scene, it can still contain errors that are carried into the next chunk. 3) New outputs then become history for later interactions, potentially reinforcing the same errors through repeated reuse. To limit this cycle, we aim to predict the future scene state from the current generated latent and the next action before generating the next interaction's video chunk. This future-state prior provides guidance for the next chunk, with the aim of reducing the errors carried forward from history. To this end, we propose a plug-and-play Causal Next-Chunk Predictor (C-NCP) with cross-model adaptability and prediction-guided modulation. We integrate C-NCP into four base generators and evaluate the augmented models across three benchmarks for interactive long-video generation and action-conditioned world modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate reduced error accumulation and improved long-horizon consistency across diverse generation settings. The project page is at https://GuangYuanKK.github.io/futureworld.

</details>


### [120] [Intrinsic Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39633)
*Deyuan Liu,Yansen Han,Kaiwen Zheng,Jun Xie,Jiacheng Liu,Zhenglin Cheng,Peng Sun,Jintao Zhang,Jianguo Li,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Intrinsic Distribution Matching (IDM)，将反向KL场分解为高斯矩分量与非高斯残差，在保留完整 DMD 校正的同时用生成器 EMA 副本显式约束特征的均值与逐坐标标准差，在少步文生图上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步/少步文生图缺乏迭代修正机会，必须精确呈现提示细节；DMD 依赖内部速度分支估计分数差，作者追问：对这部分学到的分数监督施加显式矩约束是否能进一步提升效果。

Method: 对反向 KL 场做高斯矩分量 + 非高斯残差的分解，识别可显式约束的统计量；提出 IDM：保留完整 DMD 校正项，并利用生成器自身的 EMA 副本（无需外部特征编码器）匹配特征均值与逐坐标标准差，构成额外矩损失。实验覆盖 Z-Image-Turbo 6B 与 Qwen-Image 20B 上的全参数与 LoRA 微调。

Result: 2 次网络评估下，IDM 在中文/英文 LongText-Bench 分别达 94.25% 与 90.20%，为该预算下对比方法中最高；消融验证矩损失带来增益；生成器 EMA 特征在五项指标上与外部 DINOv2 编码器表现相当。

Conclusion: 显式矩约束可作为 DMD 分数监督的有效补充，且无需外部编码器即可实现，为少步生成的分布匹配蒸馏提供了轻量、可插拔的改进方案。

Abstract: One-step and few-step text-to-image generation must accurately render the details specified in the prompt despite limited opportunities for refinement. Distribution matching distillation (DMD) uses score differences to bring the generated distribution closer to a reference distribution. Following prior work, we estimate this difference with internal velocity branches, avoiding a separate fake-score network. We ask whether an explicit moment constraint improves this learned score supervision. A decomposition of the reverse-KL field into a Gaussian moment component and a non-Gaussian residual identifies statistics that can be constrained explicitly. We introduce Intrinsic Distribution Matching (IDM), which retains the full DMD correction and matches feature means and per-coordinate standard deviations using the generator's exponential-moving-average (EMA) copy, requiring no external feature encoder. Experiments use full-parameter and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) tuning on Z-Image-Turbo 6B and Qwen-Image 20B. At two network evaluations, IDM reaches 94.25% and 90.20% on Chinese and English LongText-Bench, the highest scores among the compared methods at this budget. Controlled ablations show gains from the added moment loss, while generator EMA features perform competitively with an external DINOv2 encoder across the five reported metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/IDM.

</details>


### [121] [Where Do Vision Language Models Fail at 3D Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39639)
*Khai Le-Duc,Nguyen Kim Hai Bui,An Ngo,Nam D. Hoang,Yueqi Song,Jiarui Liu,Thai Quoc Hoang,Fang Wu,Chris Ngo,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Mona T. Diab,Ran Xu,Silvio Savarese,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 BedBench 基准，揭示视觉语言模型在 3D 物体属性（如按颜色计数）上"单项判断尚可、精确恢复与一致使用失败"的鸿沟，并主张以精确正确性、一致使用与图像依赖性评估 3D 推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 可能在单项判断上表现正确，却无法恢复并一致使用 3D 物体的精确属性；仅用准确率无法刻画这一差距，需要受控基准与更细粒度的评估维度。

Method: 构建 BedBench：13 个程序化生成的受控任务，覆盖单项判断与复合答案；评测 12 个开放权重与 4 个前沿模型；进行针对性微调、将计数作为文本输入、以及推理/扩展/视觉线索增强的对比实验；对 Qwen3.5-27B 做探针实验定位困难所在；并用移除图像的对照实验检验图像依赖性。

Result: 按颜色计数上人类 93.3%，最佳模型仅 7.2%；微调使某模型从 3.0% 提升至 68.6%，但其最常颜色相关的回答仍与自身计数不一致；将计数作为文本提供可改善判断（恢复≠一致使用）；推理、模型扩展与视觉线索仅改善单项判断；探针在 LM 输入处可恢复精确计数（65.0%），而模型回答仅 3.0%；移除图像后部分"成功"仍存在，说明缺乏视觉依赖。

Conclusion: 3D 推理评估应超越单一准确率，同时考察精确正确性、属性的一致使用与图像依赖性；当前模型在精确属性提取与一致推理上存在结构性缺陷。

Abstract: Vision-language models can get individual judgments right while struggling to recover and consistently use the exact properties of a 3D object. To characterize this gap, we introduce BedBench, a controlled benchmark of thirteen procedurally generated tasks spanning individual judgments and composite answers. Across twelve open-weight and four frontier models, per-color counting exposes a pronounced weakness: humans achieve 93.3% accuracy, compared with 7.2% for the best model. Targeted fine-tuning raises one model's accuracy from 3.0% to 68.6%, but reveals a further difficulty: its answers about the most common colors remain inconsistent with its own per-color counts. Supplying those counts as text improves its judgments, showing that recovering a property does not mean using it consistently. Reasoning, scaling, and added visual cues each improve individual judgments, but none makes exact answers reliable. To locate the difficulty, we probe Qwen3.5-27B: exact per-color counts remain recoverable at the language model's input, yet the model's answers rarely recover them (65.0% for the probe vs. 3.0% for the model). Image-removal controls further show that some apparent successes require no visual evidence. These findings motivate evaluating 3D reasoning by exact correctness, consistent use, and image dependence, rather than accuracy alone. Code and dataset are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/BedBench

</details>


### [122] [FlexTry-On: A Time-Efficient Framework for Multi-Scenario Virtual Try-on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39646)
*Phuong Dam,Quyen Nguyen Duc,Son Hoang Minh,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexTry-On：统一、高效的扩散式虚拟试衣框架，用文本感知条件提取+双条件DiT覆盖服装到人、人到人、跨域与野外场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试衣方法多为特定场景（服装到人或人到人）专用，不同设置需各自的条件策略与处理流程，缺乏统一性且推理效率不足。

Method: 采用统一条件建模：文本感知的条件提取策略将异构服装源转换为统一表示；双条件扩散Transformer联合建模服装外观与人物身份。

Result: 在服装到人与人到人基准上取得有竞争力性能，同时保持快速推理；同一框架无需单独架构或场景特定流程即可直接迁移到未见过的跨域场景。

Conclusion: 统一且高效的虚拟试衣框架具有实用价值，是迈向更灵活、更广泛适用试衣系统的一步。

Abstract: Virtual try-on has achieved remarkable progress in recent years, yet most existing methods are designed for a specific scenario, such as cloth-to-person or person-to-person try-on, and often require separate conditioning strategies and processing pipelines for different settings. We present FlexTry-On, a unified and time-efficient diffusion-based framework for diverse virtual try-on scenarios, including cloth-to-person, person-to-person, cross-domain, and in-the-wild settings. FlexTry-On adopts a common conditioning formulation, where a text-aware condition extraction strategy converts heterogeneous garment sources into a unified representation, and a dual-conditioning diffusion transformer jointly models garment appearance and person identity. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance on both cloth-to-person and person-to-person benchmarks while maintaining fast inference. Moreover, the same framework can be directly applied to unseen cross-domain scenarios without requiring separate architectures or scenario-specific processing pipelines. These results highlight the potential of unified and efficient virtual try-on frameworks as a practical step toward more flexible and widely applicable virtual try-on systems.

</details>


### [123] [Annotation-Free Representation Learning for Polyp Retrieval in Colonoscopy Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39648)
*Luca Parolari,Lamberto Ballan,Carlo Biffi,Loic Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需标注的结直肠镜视频息肉轨迹片段（tracklet）表示学习框架，直接从原始视频构建 tracklet 视图并进行时序自监督学习，在 tracklet 检索与重识别上达到 SOTA，并提出 few-shot 息肉检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜视觉评估本质上是比较性的，需要在不同视角和成像条件下获取互补视图；而现有对比方法虽能利用时序线索学习 tracklet 表示，但仍依赖人工标注的边界框，成本高、难以规模化，也无法利用无息肉帧。

Method: 提出 annotation-free 框架：直接从原始结肠镜视频构建 tracklet 视图，实现覆盖完整检查流程视频（含无可见息肉的帧）的时序自监督学习；并引入 few-shot 息肉检索任务，用少量目标息肉观测检索其剩余 tracklet。

Result: 在 REAL-Colon 数据集上，tracklet 检索与重识别均取得 SOTA，优于自监督与监督基线；在 few-shot 检索中，仅用 2 个样本即超过多数使用 10 个样本的对比方法；少量无标注检查视频即可获得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 无需人工边界框标注即可构建有效的息肉 tracklet 表示，支持全流程视频的自监督学习；few-shot 设置更贴近临床，且对数据规模要求较低，便于院内本地化模型开发。代码已开源。

Abstract: Colonoscopy is inherently comparative: reliable visual assessment of polyps often depends on complementary views acquired under different viewpoints and imaging conditions. Retrieving such views requires polyp tracklet representations, which recent contrastive methods learn from temporal cues but still build from manually annotated bounding boxes. We propose an annotation-free framework that constructs tracklet views directly from raw colonoscopy videos, enabling temporal self-supervised learning on full-procedure videos, including frames without visible polyps. On REAL-Colon, our method achieves state-of-the-art results on tracklet retrieval and re-identification, outperforming both self-supervised and supervised baselines. We then introduce few-shot polyp retrieval, a task closer to clinical use, where a few observations of a target polyp are used to retrieve its remaining tracklets: with only two examples, our method surpasses most competing methods given ten. Finally, we show that a modest number of unlabeled procedures suffices for competitive performance, making on-site model development more practical. Code is available at https://github.com/lparolari/freerep.

</details>


### [124] [Look at Where You Think: Localization-Head Alignment for Fine-Grained Perception in Thinking VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39649)
*Yuan Wang,Wei Wu,Hongning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文识别出视觉语言模型高分辨率推理的瓶颈在于“证据选择”而非“看清”，并提出定位头对齐（LHA）方法，将注意力作为训练信号，在SFT与RL中将定位头的注意力引导至关键区域，显著提升准确率并缩短推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM推理虽长却仍遗漏高分辨率图像细节；放大（zooming）方法需额外推理开销，结果奖励（outcome reward）无法告知模型“该看哪里”。作者在Qwen3.5-4B上发现瓶颈是选择而非感知。

Method: 通过头消融实验识别出少数“定位头”（localization heads）：消融其中4个比消融4个随机头多掉13.2分，且其关注随推理推进而衰减。据此提出LHA，将注意力转化为训练信号——当推理陈述视觉事实时，让定位头学习聚焦前景区域而非背景，作为SFT与RL训练中的辅助损失。

Result: 在V*、HR-Bench 4K、TreeBench上，Qwen3.5-4B准确率由74.1%提升至76.6%，为所评估的最佳单次推理4B结果，优于多轮zoom与已发布的7B模型；仅对齐即带来1.8分增益并将推理缩短20%；增益可迁移至Qwen2.5-VL-3B。

Conclusion: 高分辨率VLM推理的关键瓶颈是视觉证据的选择而非感知能力；通过对定位头的注意力对齐，可在无需额外推理开销的情况下提升精度与推理效率，并具备跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Thinking vision-language models reason for thousands of tokens, yet still miss small details in high-resolution images. Zooming methods pay for extra inference passes, and outcome rewards never tell the model where to look at. In Qwen3.5-4B, we find that the bottleneck is selection, not seeing: a few attention heads, which we call localization heads, pick the evidence out of thousands of visual tokens; ablating four of them costs 13.2 points more than ablating four random heads, and their focus fades as the reasoning grows. We therefore propose Localization-Head Alignment (LHA), which makes attention a training signal: whenever the reasoning states a visual fact, these heads learn to look at the focal region rather than the background, as an auxiliary loss in both SFT and RL training. On benchmarks of V*, HR-Bench 4K and TreeBench, LHA lifts Qwen3.5-4B from 74.1% to 76.6% accuracy, the best single-pass 4B result we evaluate, ahead of multi-pass zooming and released 7B models. Alignment alone adds 1.8 points over otherwise identical training while shortening reasoning by 20%, and the gains carry over to Qwen2.5-VL-3B.

</details>


### [125] [AB-Attention: Accurate Block Attention for Accelerating Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39663)
*Bin Xu,Mo Zhang,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Xinfeng Yang,Yixing Ma,Wei Wang,HeXin Wang,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Accurate Block Attention (AB-Attention)，在高稀疏度下准确逼近稠密 3D 注意力，在 Wan2.1 与 HunyuanVideo 上跳过 89–92% 注意力计算，PSNR 最高提升 4.4 dB，端到端加速最高 2.23×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散 Transformer 的 3D 全注意力呈二次复杂度，主导推理开销。动态块稀疏注意力虽降低成本，但在激进稀疏下保真度下降：块重要性由粗糙摘要或昂贵全局聚类得到、被剪枝块从 softmax 中消失、整块保留/丢弃忽略块内仅少数 key 重要的情况。

Method: 三项组件：(1) Intra-Block Clustering Selector (IBCS)：仅在每个 query 块内部聚类 query，不置换 token，低成本且忠实地估计块重要性；(2) Sampling with Inclusion-Probability Weighting (SIP)：按估计值比例采样块并以逆包含概率重加权，使单次前向即为稠密注意力的无偏估计而非截断；(3) 定制 kernel：将远小于 tile 的 key/value 块聚合为完整 tensor-core tile，在不增加 tensor-core 开销的前提下细化选择。

Result: 在 Wan2.1 与 HunyuanVideo 上跳过 89–92% 的注意力计算，而基线需计算 1.8–3.6× 更多；相对最强基线 PSNR 最高提升 4.4 dB，端到端加速最高 2.23×。

Conclusion: AB-Attention 是一种免训练方法，通过块内聚类估计、包含概率加权采样与细粒度 kernel 聚合，在高稀疏度下实现稠密注意力的准确近似，兼顾保真度与效率。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers generate high-quality videos, but the quadratic cost of 3D full attention dominates their inference. Dynamic block-sparse attention reduces this cost by computing exact attention only on key/value blocks selected from current activations. However, its fidelity degrades under aggressive sparsity: blocks are scored from coarse summaries or formed by costly global clustering, pruned blocks vanish from the softmax, and whole tiles are kept or dropped even when only a few of their keys matter. To address these limitations, we propose Accurate Block Attention (AB-Attention), a training-free method that accurately approximates dense attention at high sparsity. Its Intra-Block Clustering Selector (IBCS) estimates block importance faithfully yet cheaply by clustering queries only within each query block, without permuting tokens. Sampling with Inclusion-Probability Weighting (SIP) then samples blocks in proportion to these estimates and reweights them by inverse inclusion probability, so that one pass estimates dense attention rather than truncating it. Finally, its kernel gathers key/value blocks much smaller than a tile into full tensor-core tiles, refining selection at no extra tensor-core cost. On Wan2.1 and HunyuanVideo, AB-Attention skips 89--92\% of attention, whereas the baselines compute 1.8--3.6$\times$ as much, yet it still improves PSNR by up to 4.4~dB over the strongest baseline and achieves up to 2.23$\times$ end-to-end speedup. Our code is available at https://github.com/InfRec-Cardinal/AB-Attention.

</details>


### [126] [EditAgent: Rethinking Agentic Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39664)
*Haiyang Xu,Tianyu Wang,Jialu Li,Jiteng Mu,Yuanjun Xiong,Jianming Zhang,Zhuowen Tu,Mingze Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditAgent 通过同时扩展智能体图像编辑的动作空间与观察空间，使失败步骤可路由到通用/专用工具并附带验证记录供 VLM 判别，显著优于现有编辑器与顺序精修策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体式图像编辑器在步骤失败后仅用狭窄工具集重试，且评判器仅靠肉眼直接打分，受限于两方面：动作空间（能做什么）与观察空间（评判器能看到什么）。实测表明通用编辑器在 40% 以上案例中无可用于结果，且无判别器在各编辑类型上稳定可靠。

Method: 提出 EditAgent：(1) 将每个步骤路由到通用编辑器与专用方法，扩大动作空间；(2) 为每个候选输出附加验证记录（verification record），扩大观察空间；(3) 候选并行生成，并一次性进行 listwise 评判。

Result: 借助验证记录，现成 VLM 判别器优于微调奖励模型；专家在 78.5% 的有效比较中更偏好该并行+listwise 方案而非顺序精修，且判别调用不超过其一半；在两个开源权重编辑器、三个基准上均取得提升，并可作为数据引擎训练更强编辑器。

Conclusion: 智能体图像编辑的能力瓶颈在于动作空间与观察空间的联合约束；同步扩展二者并配合并行候选生成与 listwise 验证式评判，可高效提升编辑成功率与质量，同时充当高质量数据生产引擎。

Abstract: Agentic image editors decompose complex requests into steps, but when a step fails, they mostly retry with the same narrow set of tools and a judge that scores each output by eye directly. We argue that such agents are bounded by two spaces: the action space of what the agent can do, and the observation space of what its judge can see. On our expert-annotated benchmark, a general-purpose editor yields no usable result in over 40% of the cases, and no judge is reliable across edit types. We present EditAgent, which expands both spaces. It routes each step to general editors and specialized methods, and attaches to each candidate a verification record. With these records, off-the-shelf VLM judges outperform finetuned reward models. Candidates are generated in parallel and judged in one listwise call: experts prefer this to sequential refinement in 78.5% of decided comparisons, at no more than half the judge calls. EditAgent improves two open-weight editors on three benchmarks and serves as a data engine for training stronger editors.

</details>


### [127] [DEQ-Stereo: Deep Euilibrium Video Stereo Matching via Adaptive Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39701)
*Kiljoon Han,Kyumin Hwang,Wonheok Choi,Jeonghoon Kim,Sunghoom Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DEQ-Stereo，基于深度均衡（DEQ）范式的视频立体匹配框架，将迭代细化重构为隐式不动点问题并自适应终止，配合ConvSGU保证快速稳定收敛、TST实现跨帧热启动，在精度-延迟权衡上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代细化立体匹配模型采用固定更新次数，计算开销大且速度-精度权衡僵化；在视频流中该问题被放大，在线方法只能减少特征通道或依赖昂贵的时序warping。

Method: 1) 将细化过程建模为隐式不动点问题，收敛即终止，替代固定迭代展开；2) 提出卷积简化门控单元ConvSGU，将隐状态建模为输入的逐元素门控求和，去除冗余非线性以加速并稳定帧内收敛；3) 提出时序状态传递TST，将收敛隐状态热启动至下一帧，无需显式位姿或warping先验即可利用跨帧时序一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明DEQ-Stereo达到SOTA性能，并具有更优的精度-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: DEQ-Stereo通过隐式不动点求解与时序热启动，在无需显式warping的前提下兼顾视频立体匹配的精度与效率，为自适应迭代细化提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Iterative refinement models have significantly improved stereo matching accuracy, but their rigid, fixed update schedules incur heavy computational costs and a strict trade-off between speed and accuracy. This challenge is magnified in video streams, where traditional online methods resort to reducing feature channels or relying on expensive temporal warping. To address this, we propose \textbf{DEQ-Stereo}, a novel video stereo matching framework based on the Deep Equilibrium (DEQ) paradigm. Instead of unrolling a fixed number of iterations, DEQ-Stereo reformulates the refinement as an implicit fixed-point problem, adaptively terminating once convergence is met. To ensure fast and stable intra-frame convergence, we introduce the \textbf{Convolutional Simplified Gated Unit (ConvSGU)}, which eliminates unnecessary non-linearities by modeling the latent state as a gated, element-wise sum of inputs. Furthermore, to exploit cross-frame temporal coherence, we propose \textbf{Temporal State Transfer (TST)}, a warm-start strategy that propagates the converged hidden state to the next frame without requiring explicit pose or warping priors. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that DEQ-Stereo delivers state-of-the-art performance with a superior accuracy-to-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [128] [Confidence Filtering in Video-Based Gait Assessment: Measurement Error versus Selection Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39704)
*David Regev,Eran Beeri Bamani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究揭示基于视频的步态评估中"高置信度姿态筛选"会引入选择偏移，小样本中临床误差不变并不能证明筛选保持了所评估的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频步态评估常仅保留高置信度姿态观测以降低测量误差，但此举可能改变被评估的动作本身；作者希望区分"测量误差下降"与"选择偏移"这两种效应，并检验常用评价指标（临床误差）能否识别该风险。

Method: 在帕金森相关队列（n=96）与NF2相关复制队列（n=18）中，将置信度筛选与预算匹配的随机筛选对比；再基于队列的窗口结构对测量误差与选择偏移进行分解与模拟，并考察保持运动组比例的筛选策略。

Result: 帕金森队列中置信度筛选使观测动作汇总产生小而一致的偏移（+0.05 SD，95% CI 0.03–0.07），而留出集Mini-BESTest误差基本不变（-0.04分，-0.13–0.05）；保持运动组比例的筛选策略减小了偏移；NF2队列无偏移且其预测器未优于常数基线。置信度与动作仅弱相关（rho=0.14）；若系统误差仅在个体内重分布，筛选可引入高达0.58度的偏差。

Conclusion: 当误差依赖置信度时，筛选可降低局部误差；但一旦置信度更多追踪动作而非误差，就会使终点估计产生偏移。因此，小队列预测器中"临床误差不变"不能作为质量筛选保持所评估动作的证据。

Abstract: Video-based gait assessment often keeps only high-confidence pose observations, which can lower measurement error but change the movement assessed. We compared confidence selection with budget-matched random selection in a Parkinson-related cohort (n = 96) and an NF2-related replication cohort (n = 18). In the Parkinson-related cohort, confidence selection produced a small but consistent shift in the observed movement summary (+0.05 SD, 95% CI 0.03 to 0.07), while held-out Mini-BESTest error stayed flat (-0.04 points, -0.13 to 0.05); a policy preserving motion-group proportions reduced the shift, while NF2 showed no shift and its predictor did not beat a constant baseline. Because this shift mixes measurement error with selection shift, we decompose and simulate both on the cohort's window structure. Filtering lowers local error when error depends on confidence, but biases the endpoint once confidence tracks movement more than error. In the Parkinson-related data, confidence was only weakly tied to movement (rho = 0.14). There, filtering is harmless if well-tracked windows are unbiased. If instead systematic error only redistributes within participants, filtering can add up to 0.58 degrees. Unchanged clinical error from such small-cohort predictors therefore cannot show that quality filtering preserves the assessed movement.

</details>


### [129] [NIF: Normal-Intersection Fields for Geometrically Consistent Oriented Box Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39715)
*Shaokai Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NIF细化模块，用20个边界锚点的法向有符号距离场替代二次框回归，经可靠性加权最小二乘解析恢复精化旋转框，仅增0.132M参数，在DOTA-v1.0等数据集取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归头从池化区域表示直接预测中心、尺寸、角度等耦合参数，这些参数的小误差会使长边整体位移，即使分类正确也难以保证框贴合物体边界。

Method: 给定粗定向框，在20个边界锚点处观测局部特征，预测沿外法向的有符号距离；这些观测在五个几何变量下具有精确仿射表示，用可微、可靠性加权的最小二乘拟合将预测场投影到该表示上，解析恢复精化矩形；配合切向池化保持法向位置剖面，共享投影结构控制参数增量。

Result: DOTA-v1.0上配合Oriented R-CNN、LSKNet-S、Strip R-CNN分别达78.00%、79.11%、79.02% mAP，为单尺度SOTA；DIOR-R达68.19%；FAIR1M/DIOR-R基线提升0.30/0.68%；HRSC平均IoU由0.867升至0.888，场误差降24.5%；四种冻结检测器框架下相对AP75提升1.57–4.18%，COCO式mAP提升0.42–3.74%，无需重训检测器。

Conclusion: 以法向交点场+解析最小二乘拟合替代框回归，可在极低参数开销、检测器冻结条件下稳定提升定向检测精度，具良好通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Accurate oriented detection requires a box to follow object boundaries, yet a standard regression head predicts its center, size, and angle from a pooled region representation. Small errors in these coupled parameters can displace long boundaries even when the object is correctly classified. We propose NIF, a refinement module that replaces an additional box regressor with normal-intersection fields. Given a coarse oriented box, the module observes local features at 20 boundary anchors and predicts signed distances along their outward normals. These observations admit an exact affine representation in five geometric variables. A differentiable, reliability-weighted least-squares fit projects the predicted field onto this representation and analytically recovers a refined rectangle. Tangential pooling preserves the normal position profile, while shared projection limits the added network to 0.132M parameters. On DOTA-v1.0, NIF reaches 78.00%, 79.11%, and 79.02% mAP with Oriented R-CNN, LSKNet-S, and Strip R-CNN, respectively, achieving the highest single-scale SOTA result in Table 1. It reaches 68.19% on DIOR-R, improves matched FAIR1M/DIOR-R baselines by 0.30/0.68%, and raises HRSC mean IoU from 0.867 to 0.888 while reducing field error by 24.5%. Across four different detector frameworks with the detectors frozen, relative AP75 improves by 1.57-4.18% and COCO-style mAP by 0.42-3.74%, without detector retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/zsk466991125-collab/Normal-Intersection-Fields.

</details>


### [130] [WorldProbe: When Video World Models Fall Apart under Long-Horizon Spatial Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39721)
*Minqi Wang,Jie Peng,Hejun Dong,Ruchang Yao,Puyi Wang,Siyuan Huang,Jihai Zhang,Yafu Li,Qi Dou,Xiaoye Qu,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldProbe，一个用目标条件多模态智能体在生成过程中在线调整相机动作的诊断性基准，用于评测视频世界模型在长时程、大范围相机运动探索下的持久3D世界一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型基准多只评测短程交互，或使用生成前固定的相机轨迹，因而无法揭示模型在长时程、大范围相机运动探索下的表现，长程记忆与持久世界一致性缺乏有效诊断手段。

Method: 构建WorldProbe基准：包含164个测试用例、划分为四个探测族（probing families）；采用目标条件（goal-conditioned）多模态智能体，在生成过程中依据目标在线自适应选择相机动作，实现闭环探索式评测；从四个维度进行度量。

Result: 在10个SOTA模型上共生成1600余条rollout、总计约800分钟、时程最长180秒；评测显示局部指标随时间退化，并出现长程不一致；近期模型在视觉与内容质量上维持更久，但长程记忆与世界演化能力仍较弱。

Conclusion: 揭示了"局部稳定生成"与"持久世界一致性"之间的差距，该差距被现有基准大幅忽略；WorldProbe为长时程闭环探索下的世界模型评测提供了诊断工具（代码与资源已开源）。

Abstract: Video world models generate future visual observations from camera or action inputs, with the goal of synthesizing persistent 3D worlds that can be explored across time and space. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate short interactions or rely on camera trajectories fixed before generation. The performance of video world models under long-horizon exploration with extensive camera motion remains under-explored. We introduce WorldProbe, a diagnostic benchmark that uses a goal-conditioned multimodal agent to adapt camera actions online during generation. WorldProbe comprises 164 test cases across four probing families. Across 10 state-of-the-art models, we generate more than 1,600 rollouts totaling approximately 800 minutes, with horizons up to 180 seconds. Our evaluation across four dimensions reveals both the degradation of local measures and the emergence of long-range inconsistencies. We find that recent models sustain visual and content quality for longer, while long-range memory and evolution remain weak. These results expose a gap between locally stable generation and persistent world consistency that is largely missed by existing benchmarks. Code and benchmark resources are available at https://github.com/Untitled-W/WorldProbe.

</details>


### [131] [Cross-Encoder Anchor Refinement for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39727)
*Kaisong Ji,Ziyang Xu,Chang Bian,Yu Zhou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Encoder Anchor Refinement (CEAR)，在锚点阶段连接双编码路径，融合独立卷积编码器构建的图像特定记忆与ViT patch特征，实现跨域零样本异常检测与细粒度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本异常检测需将辅助训练域学到的异常判别能力迁移到未见类别并定位细微缺陷；核心难点在于构建结合上下文表征与互补局部证据的判别性参考。

Method: 在正常/异常原型构建之前的锚点阶段连接两条视觉编码路径：以工业产品识别预训练的独立卷积编码器构建跨选定ViT层的图像特定记忆；空间锚点从ViT patch特征读取并通过交叉注意力查询该记忆，经残差精炼；随后由图像条件化的正常与异常查询对锚点聚合进行门控，构建用于异常评分的原型。

Result: 在13个工业与医学数据集上，CEAR在68项评估（图像级检测+像素级定位）中57项最优；44项像素级比较中41项超越所有SOTA；11个定位数据集上像素级max-F1与AP平均提升4.45与3.79个百分点。

Conclusion: 在锚点阶段引入跨编码器交互可有效整合互补视觉表征，实现跨域细粒度异常定位。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection aims to transfer anomaly discrimination from auxiliary training domains to unseen categories while localizing subtle defects. A key challenge is to construct discriminative references that combine contextual representations with complementary local evidence. We present Cross-Encoder Anchor Refinement (CEAR), which connects two visual encoding paths for the same image at the anchor stage, before normal and anomalous prototypes are constructed. An independent convolutional encoder pretrained for industrial product recognition builds an image-specific memory shared across selected vision transformer (ViT) layers. Spatial anchors read from ViT patch features query this memory through cross-attention and undergo residual refinement. Image-conditioned normal and anomalous queries then gate anchor aggregation to construct prototypes for anomaly scoring. Across 13 industrial and medical datasets, CEAR achieves the best performance in 57 of 68 evaluations spanning image-level detection and pixel-level localization. Notably, CEAR surpasses all other state-of-the-art methods in 41 of 44 pixel-level comparisons. Across 11 localization datasets, mean gains in pixel-level maximum F1 score and average precision are 4.45 and 3.79 percentage points, respectively. This study shows how cross-encoder interaction at the anchor stage can integrate complementary visual representations for fine-grained anomaly localization across domains.

</details>


### [132] [SCOPE: Semantic COdec Pre-Editing for Object-Preserving Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39778)
*Silvia Dani,Leonardo Galteri,Marco Bertini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种编码前图像预编辑模型，以编解码器族与压缩强度为条件，在压缩前保护任务相关区域、简化背景，从而改善机器视觉的码率-检测精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图像编解码器主要面向人眼感知优化，而非下游机器视觉任务精度；而在视觉系统前置压缩的场景下，需兼顾低码率与检测精度。

Method: 提出条件式预编辑器SCOPE，输入图像在压缩前被修改：以编解码器类别和压缩强度为条件，单一网络适配多种压缩设置，无需重训或加载专用权重；学习有选择性地保留任务相关区域、简化不重要的背景以提升可压缩性。

Result: 在多种编解码器族与检测器架构上，率-精度曲线均获得一致提升；并对未见过的学习式与基于小波的压缩族表现出较强迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过任务感知的压缩前预编辑可有效改善码率-精度权衡，且条件化设计赋予单一模型跨编解码器与跨架构的通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Image compression is increasingly used before machine vision systems, yet standard codecs are mainly optimized for human perception rather than downstream task accuracy. We propose SCOPE (Semantic COdec Pre-Editing), a conditional image pre-editor that modifies images before compression to improve the bitrate and detection accuracy trade-off. The model is conditioned on the codec family and compression severity, allowing a single trained network to adapt to multiple compression settings without retraining or loading codec-specific weights. Instead of uniformly preserving the whole image, the pre-editor learns to protect task-relevant regions while simplifying less important background content to improve compressibility. The resulting rate-accuracy curves show consistent gains across different codec families and detector architectures, together with strong transferability to unseen learned and wavelet-based compression families.

</details>


### [133] [GROME: GROunding Factorized MEmory into Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39812)
*Fang Yu,Xiaoxue Wu,Haiyu Zhang,Yangfu Li,Botong Zhao,Yu Qiao,Tianfan Xue,Yue Lu,Xinyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GROME 分解式记忆框架，通过静态-动态因子分解、局部记忆检索与免训练记忆注入，使视频世界模型在上下文窗口外仍保持静态场景与动态实体的长程一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当观测内容超出上下文窗口后，视频世界模型的长期一致性难以维持。隐式检索式记忆受限于上下文容量且静态与动态信息纠缠；显式全局表示式记忆难以建模演化动态并累积几何误差。

Method: 三组件协同：(1) 静态-动态因子分解，将历史证据解耦为静态场景几何与动态实体状态；(2) 局部记忆检索，选取空间对齐的静态历史以消除几何漂移，并取最新动态实体状态保证时间连续；(3) 免训练记忆注入，经原生条件通路将因子化记忆注入冻结骨干模型。

Result: 长程重访场景实验中，相比现有方法显著提升场景持久性与动态实体保真度，同时保持冻结骨干的视觉质量与可控性，无需额外训练；并可即插即用地接入外部观测。

Conclusion: 因子化记忆是维持长程一致性的有效路径；免训练注入使方法具备即插即用性，可扩展至外部观测驱动的可控视频续写与场景探索。

Abstract: Long-horizon consistency is essential for video world models, yet it becomes difficult to maintain once observed content falls outside the context window. Existing memory mechanisms either retrieve history as implicit context, which remains constrained by context capacity and entangles static and dynamic information or consolidate history into explicit global representations, which struggle to model evolving dynamics and accumulate geometric errors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce \textbf{GROME}, a novel factorized memory framework that augments video world models for long-horizon coherence across both static scenes and dynamic entities. GROME operates via three synergistic components: 1)~\emph{Static-Dynamic Factorization} decouples historical evidence into static and dynamic components, preserving static scene geometry and dynamic entity states separately; 2)~\emph{Local Memory Retrieval} selects spatially aligned static history to eliminate geometric drift alongside the latest dynamic entity states to ensure temporal continuity; 3)~\emph{Training-Free Memory Injection} seamlessly grounds the factorized memory into video world models via their native conditioning pathways. Experiments on long-horizon revisit scenarios demonstrate that GROME substantially enhances scene persistence and dynamic entity fidelity over existing methods, all while preserving the visual quality and controllability of the frozen backbone without additional training. Beyond self-rollouts, GROME readily accommodates external observations as plug-and-play memory, unlocking flexible and controllable video continuation and scene exploration.

</details>


### [134] [Aligning Medical Vision Language Models at the Capability Boundary with Entropy Adaptive Rubric Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39835)
*Hongyu Bai,Jiaqi Zeng,Chunzheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedCBR两阶段对齐框架，结合边界感知偏好优化（DBPO）与基于评分量规的强化学习（EGPO），提升医学视觉语言模型的诊断推理准确性与可验证的解剖/逻辑支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型的对齐目标激励了错误行为：统一监督微调使简单样本主导更新、关键边界病例过早饱和；仅基于最终答案正确性的稀疏奖励无法评估解剖定位与临床逻辑，并诱发通过答案捷径的奖励黑客。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 利用经验通过率在模型能力前沿构造反事实边界偏好对（共享推理前缀、在决定性诊断分支处分叉），用难度加权边界偏好优化（DBPO）将学习集中于校准后的边界区域；(2) 从边界轨迹中蒸馏可复用临床评分量规，依据预测熵进行门控激活，采用熵门控接地的策略优化（EGPO），奖励答案正确性、解剖定位与推理忠实度，并在可靠样本上抑制量规噪声；以边界对齐模型作为参考策略，耦合群体级边界先验与实例级不确定性。

Result: 在六个医学基准上一致优于强通用、专用及强化学习基线，平均准确率达61.2%，在困难鉴别诊断任务上提升最大；消融显示两阶段组合具有超加性增益，超过各自改进之和。

Conclusion: 将边界感知偏好优化与量规接地强化学习相结合，可使医学诊断推理更准确且更具临床可验证性，且两阶段协同效应显著。

Abstract: Medical diagnostic reasoning requires verifiable links between visual evidence and clinical knowledge, yet current medical vision language models are aligned with objectives that reward the wrong behavior. Uniform supervised fine tuning lets easy cases dominate updates while clinically critical boundary cases saturate prematurely. Sparse rewards based only on final-answer correctness cannot assess anatomical grounding or clinical logic, inviting reward hacking through answer shortcuts. We propose MedCBR, a two-stage alignment framework coupling boundary-aware preference optimization with rubric-grounded reinforcement learning. First, we construct counterfactual boundary preferences at the model's capability frontier using empirical pass rates and preference pairs that share reasoning prefixes but diverge at decisive diagnostic branches. Difficulty Weighted Boundary Preference Optimization (DBPO) concentrates learning on this calibrated boundary region. Second, we distill reusable clinical rubrics from boundary trajectories and activate them according to prediction entropy. Entropy Gated Grounded Policy Optimization (EGPO) rewards answer correctness, anatomical grounding, and reasoning fidelity while suppressing rubric noise on reliable samples. A reference policy anchored to the boundary-aligned model couples population-level boundary priors with instance-level uncertainty, making diagnostic reasoning more accurate and clinically grounded. Experiments on six medical benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong general, specialized, and reinforcement learning baselines, reaching 61.2% average accuracy with the largest gains on challenging differential diagnosis tasks. Ablations show super-additive gains: combining the two stages exceeds the sum of their individual improvements.

</details>


### [135] [LiDAR-SHIELD: A Fully Dockerized Adversarial Robustness Assessment Framework for LiDAR-Based 3D Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39848)
*Alexandros Gkillas,Ioulia Kapsali,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Aris S. Lalos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LIDARSHIELD 是一个统一且完全 Docker 化的 LiDAR 对抗攻击框架，通过三个轻量级 HTTP 端点（/info、/forward、/gradient）将任意 3D 模型接入攻击引擎，实现跨架构、跨任务的系统性对抗鲁棒性评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR 对抗攻击实现分散于各自仓库，与特定架构或任务绑定，需要手动重建环境并编写模型专用集成代码，导致安全压力测试难以扩展到异构 LiDAR 主干网络。作者希望反转工作流：不再把目标模型安装进工具箱，而是让工具箱以通用协议对接任意模型。

Method: 框架采用引擎-服务（engine-service）协议，将模型通过三个 HTTP 端点暴露：/info（查询模型信息）、/forward（前向推理）、/gradient（梯度回传）。接入新模型或数据集只需一个 loader 函数或适配器。同一协议同时支持 3D 语义分割与 3D 目标检测，并集成任务感知的攻击套件：分割任务含 PGD 与 Square Attack，检测任务含点扰动、点删除、点注入与 LiDAttack。整个系统完全 Docker 化。

Result: 框架能够以统一接口对异构 LiDAR 主干网络（backbone）发起多种最先进攻击，系统性地暴露角例与脆弱性，且新模型/数据集接入成本极低（单个 loader 或适配器）。代码已开源（GitHub: alexandrosgk/LiDAR-shield）。

Conclusion: LIDARSHIELD 以模型解耦的端点式架构降低了 LiDAR 对抗攻击的复现与扩展门槛，为自动驾驶与机器人 3D 感知模型的部署前安全评测提供了可复现、可扩展的统一平台。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D perception underpins autonomous driving and robotics, yet deploying these models safely requires stress-testing them against adversarial inputs before real-world use. Although state-of-the-art adversarial attacks exist, their implementations are often fragmented across individual repositories, each tied to a specific architecture or task and requiring manual environment reconstruction and model-specific integration code. We present LIDARSHIELD, a unified and fully Dockerized framework that reverses this workflow: rather than installing the target model inside the toolkit, LIDARSHIELD exposes any model through three lightweight HTTP endpoints, namely /info, /forward, and /gradient As a result, connecting a new model or dataset requires only a single loader function or adapter. The framework supports both 3D semantic segmentation and 3D object detection through the same engine-service protocol, integrating a task-aware suite of state-of-the-art attacks, including PGD and Square Attack for segmentation, and point perturbation, point removal, point injection, and LiDAttack for detection. This enables systematic exposure of corner cases and vulnerabilities across heterogeneous LiDAR backbones. The LiDAR-Shield codebase is publicly available at https://github.com/alexandrosgk/LiDAR-shield.

</details>


### [136] [Open-set multi-view seahorse re-identification from underwater photograph sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39857)
*Tristan Fauvel,Sylvie Louisy,Patrick Louisy,Thomas Fauvel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于图像序列的海马分类、性别判定与开集重识别方法，在35,914张、474个个体的野外数据上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动动物重识别可支撑大规模、微创的捕获-标记-重捕获研究，但同一次观测中拍摄的照片质量差异大，单张图像常不足以提供可靠重识别所需的信息，因此需利用序列中的多图信息。

Method: 构建含35,914张照片、474个海马个体的野生数据集；提出针对图像序列的物种（分类学）分类、性别判定和开集重识别新方法，核心在于融合多张照片的信息进行决策。

Result: 多图信息融合的方法在分类、性别判定与开集重识别任务上均取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 利用观测序列中的多张照片可有效克服单图质量波动问题，为可扩展的微创CMR研究提供可行方案。

Abstract: Automated animal re-identification from photograph sequences can facilitate minimally invasive Capture-Mark-Recapture studies at scale. Yet, photographs taken during an observation session can vary greatly in quality, not always providing the necessary information for accurate re-identification. In this paper, we present new methods for taxonomic classification, sex determination, and open-set re-identification from image sequences. We train and evaluate our models on a dataset of 35,914 photographs of 474 individual seahorses observed in the wild. We demonstrate that our approach, by combining information from multiple photographs, yields state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [137] [M-PFT: Angular Margin for Calibrated Prompt-Tuning of Fine-Tuned Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39861)
*Shihab Aaqil Ahamed,Mohamed Ansaff,Nang Khin Myat Lay Phyu,Udaya Sampath K. Perera Miriya Thanthrige,Chamira U. S. Edussooriya,Ranga Rodrigo,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M-PFT 是一种面向 prompt-tuning VLM 的角度间隔方法，通过两个互补目标扩大类间角度间隔，缓解 base-to-novel 双重校准误差，并保持预训练语义几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prompt-tuned VLM 存在 base-to-novel 双重失配校准问题：base 类欠自信、novel 类过自信。现有校准方法或单独、或联合处理，但缺乏对超球嵌入空间的几何理解，未能利用角度判别性来修正扭曲的决策边界。

Method: 提出 M-PFT：在超球嵌入空间中引入角度间隔，包含两个互补目标——1）最小化可训练类嵌入之间的最大余弦相似度，减少嵌入坍缩；2）最小化每个可训练嵌入与其对应 zero-shot 嵌入的角度距离，保持语义几何。

Result: 在 15 个数据集、9 种 prompt-tuning 方法及多种 backbone 上验证：降低 novel 类平均校准误差，base 类保持竞争力，准确率相当；分析表明可有效修正 prompt tuning 导致的决策边界扭曲，同时保留预训练嵌入空间，计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 角度间隔约束可在不牺牲准确率的前提下改善 prompt-tuned VLM 的双重校准问题，且具备通用性与低开销特性。

Abstract: Prompt-tuning has emerged as a parameter-efficient technique for adapting large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) to downstream tasks while keeping the pre-trained weights frozen. However, prompt-tuned VLMs often suffer from a base-to-novel dual miscalibration problem, where models typically show underconfidence on base classes and overconfidence on novel classes. Current VLM calibration approaches address this dual miscalibration either separately or jointly, but lack a geometric understanding of the hyperspherical embedding space and thus fail to exploit angular discriminative properties to adjust distorted decision boundaries. To address this, we propose M-PFT, a novel angular-margin approach for prompt tuning that introduces two complementary objectives to promote class discriminability and semantic anchoring. This enlarges the angular margin by 1) minimizing the maximum cosine similarity between tunable class embeddings to reduce embedding collapse and 2) minimizing the angular distance between each tunable embedding and its corresponding zero-shot embedding to preserve semantic geometry. Extensive experiments across 9 prompt-tuning methods with various backbones on 15 diverse datasets show that our approach reduces the average calibration error on novel classes, while remaining competitive on base classes and maintaining comparable accuracy. Further analyses reveal that M-PFT effectively adjusts distorted decision boundaries induced by prompt tuning while preserving the pre-trained embedding space, improving calibration with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [138] [Parametric Gaussian Noise Mixer (PGNM): Unified Synthesis of Camera Noise and Film Grain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39868)
*Mingyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种介于物理成像模型与神经网络噪声生成器之间的显式统计噪声合成模型：用归一化卷积将高斯场在像素与颜色通道间相关，再用查找表把高斯值映射为给定干净强度下的残差；两部分因场保持标准高斯而可独立拟合，无需复制物理过程或训练生成网络。在SIDD与Newson胶片颗粒上均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有噪声合成主要有两条路线：基于图像形成过程的物理模型，以及训练神经网络直接生成噪声图像。前者受限于建模假设，后者依赖大量训练且难以解释。作者希望寻找中间方案——直接从成对的干净/噪声图像拟合一个显式统计模型，兼具可解释性、简洁性与生成效率。

Method: （1）归一化卷积：对高斯随机场在空间像素和颜色通道上进行相关，刻画噪声的空间与通道相关性；（2）查找表映射：以干净图像强度为条件，将每个高斯值映射为对应的噪声残差，建模信号依赖的噪声方差。由于相关后的场仍为标准高斯分布，卷积相关部分与查找映射部分可分开独立拟合，无需复现物理成像过程，也无需训练神经生成器。同一套公式同时用于SIDD相机噪声与Newson模拟胶片颗粒。

Result: 在SIDD上，该方法在所评估方法中取得最低的平均分布误差与相关性误差；在Newson胶片颗粒上保持有竞争力。用其合成噪声训练的降噪器性能最接近用真实噪声训练的降噪器。1080p帧在GPU上渲染耗时约10 ms。

Conclusion: 显式统计建模可在不依赖物理过程复现与神经生成器的前提下，高质量、可解释且高效地合成真实噪声，并在分布匹配、相关性刻画与下游降噪任务上验证了其有效性，为噪声合成提供了一条实用中间路径。

Abstract: Noise synthesis is commonly approached through physical models of image formation or neural networks trained to generate noisy images. We explore a middle ground: an explicit statistical model fitted directly from paired clean and noisy images. A normalized convolution correlates a Gaussian field across pixels and color channels, and a lookup maps each Gaussian value to a residual given the clean intensity. Because the field stays standard Gaussian, the two components are fitted separately, without reproducing the physical process or training a neural generator.
